## פ (id: BDB07546)

פ, פּ, ף, *Pê*, seventeenth letter; used as numeral 8o in post B. Hebrew.

> \[Str6311] פֹּא v. פֹּה

* **Associated Articles:** פֹּה (ID: `BDB07584@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## פּאה (id: BDB07548)

\[Str6284] † \[פּאה] **vb.** perh. (si vera l.) cleave in pieces (Ar. (فأى, فأو) فَاأَ *cleave, split* (head, bowl, etc.); Sab. פעי DHMZMG xxx (1876\), 701 f.);—**Hiph.** *Impf.* 1 s. sf. אַפְאֵיהֶם [Dt 32:26](https://ref.ly/Deut32:26) *I* \[´י] *will cleave them in pieces* (poss. point ´אֶפ, i.e. **Qal**; on other views v. Dr; rd. perh. אֲַפִיצֵם LXX Gr).

> \[Str6285] פֵּאָה:86 **n. f.** corner, side (*part cut off,* ‘Abschnitt,’ acc. to Schröter in MeArchiv. i. 461 BaZMG xii (1887\), 615 BuhlLex, but perh. bilit., NöM. 485 Sta§ 185, and not fr. √ פאה; cf. Ar. *company,* etc. (Nöl. e.); As. *pâtu, side, edge, border,* v. esp. MeissnSuppl. 74; NH \= BH, Aram. פָּאתָא, ܦܰܐܬܳܐ);—abs. ´פ [Je 9:25](https://ref.ly/Jer9:25) \+ 4 t.; cstr. פְּאַת [Am 3:12](https://ref.ly/Amos3:12) \+ 77 t.; pl. abs. פֵּאֹת [Ex 25:26](https://ref.ly/Exod25:26), [37:23](https://ref.ly/Exod37:23); du. cstr. פַּאֲַתֵי [Nu 24:17](https://ref.ly/Num24:17) (but read prob. פְּאַת as ‖ [Je 48:45](https://ref.ly/Jer48:45));—† **1\.** *corner* of divan [Am 3:12](https://ref.ly/Amos3:12); table [Ex 25:26](https://ref.ly/Exod25:26), [37:23](https://ref.ly/Exod37:23); field [Lv 19:9](https://ref.ly/Lev19:9), [23:22](https://ref.ly/Lev23:22) (H); land (prob.) וַתַּחְלְקֵם לְפֵאָה [Ne 9:22](https://ref.ly/Neh9:22), i.e. into every corner; ראֹשְׁכֶם ´פ [Lv 19:27](https://ref.ly/Lev19:27) (i.e. *your temples*), זְקֶָ\+נְ ´פ v:[27](https://ref.ly/Lev19:27) cf. [21:5](https://ref.ly/Lev21:5) (all H); פֵאָה קְצוּצֵי *those clipped on the temples* [Je 9:25](https://ref.ly/Jer9:25), [25:23](https://ref.ly/Jer25:23), [49:32](https://ref.ly/Jer49:32) (epith. of Arab. tribes, Herod.:iii, 8 WMMAs. u. Eur. 140 f. WeSkizzen iii. 119; Heid. 2, 198\); *temples of Moab* (under fig. of man) [Nu 24:17](https://ref.ly/Num24:17) (JE) [Je 48:45](https://ref.ly/Jer48:45). **2\.** *side:* **a.** usu. of Ezekiel's temple, the tabern., the holy city and the land, or its subdivisions (only Ez 41–48 \[47 t.] and P \[26 t.]): in plur. (ה)פְּאַת קָדִ֫ימ *East side* Ez [47:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:18) \+ 17 t. Ez, \= ´קֵדְמָה פ [Ex 27:13](https://ref.ly/Exod27:13) \+ 3 t. P and Ez [45:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:7) (v. Co Toy); ´פ (ה)צָפוֺנ *North side* Ez [47:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:15), [Ex 26:20](https://ref.ly/Exod26:20) \+ 9 t.; נָגָב ´פ (ֶ נ֫גְבָּה *South side* Ez [47:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:19), [47:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:19), [Ex 27:9](https://ref.ly/Exod27:9) \+ 8 t.; יָם ´פ (יָ֫מָּה) *West side* Ez [45:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:7), [Ex 27:12](https://ref.ly/Exod27:12) \+ 21 t. \+ דֶּרֶ־ח ´הַיָּם פ Ez [41:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:12) (also קָדִים הַיָּם ´פ 48:1\); abs. only לַפֵּאָה הָאֶחָת [Ex 27:9](https://ref.ly/Exod27:9). **b.** פְּאַת פָּנָיו [Lv 13:41](https://ref.ly/Lev13:41) *side* (border) *of his face* (forehead and temples; P).

## פָּאַר (id: BDB07551)

\[Str6286] † 1\. \[פָּאַר] **vb. Pi.** beautify, glorify;—*Pf.* 3 ms. sf. פֵּאֲרָח (obj. Isr.) [Is 55:5](https://ref.ly/Isa55:5), [60:9](https://ref.ly/Isa60:9); c. acc. of temple; *Impf.* 1 s. אֲפָאֵר [60:7](https://ref.ly/Isa60:7); *Inf. cstr.* פָּאֵר v:[13](https://ref.ly/Isa60:13), [Ezr 7:27](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:27); so פָּאֵר עֲנָוִים בִּישׁוּעָה [Psalm 149:4](https://ref.ly/Ps149:4). **Hithp. 1\.** *glorify oneself,* c. עַל Pers.: \= boast, *Impf.* 3 ms. יִתְפָּאֵר Ju [7:2](https://ref.ly/Judg7:2), [Is 10:15](https://ref.ly/Isa10:15); as polite address to king, *Imv.* ms. הִתְפָּאֵר [Ex 8:5](https://ref.ly/Exod8:5) (J), *assume the honour over me* (to decide) *when,* etc. **2\.** *get glory to oneself, be glorified, by means of* (ב), of ´י, בְּיִשְרָאֵל יִתְפָּאָר [Is 44:23](https://ref.ly/Isa44:23), so 1 s. אֶתְפָּאָר [49:3](https://ref.ly/Isa49:3); of people \[by ´י], *Inf. cstr.* הִתְפָּאֵר [60:21](https://ref.ly/Isa60:21), [61:3](https://ref.ly/Isa61:3).

> \[Str6287] † פְּאֵר **n.m.**:Ez [44:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:18) head\-dress, turban (? orig. *ornament*);—abs. ´פ of bridegroom [Is 61:10](https://ref.ly/Isa61:10), sign of joy v:[3](https://ref.ly/Isa61:3) (opp. mourning, and so) פְּאֵרְ Ez [24:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:17), pl. sf. פְּאֵרֵכֶם v:[23](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:23) (worn by men of position); of priests פַּאֲרֵי פִשְׁתִּים Ez [44:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:18), ´הַמִּנְבָּעֹת שֵׁשׁפּ [Ex 39:28](https://ref.ly/Exod39:28) (P); pl. abs. פְּאֵרִים [Is 3:20](https://ref.ly/Isa3:20) (of luxurious women).

> \[Str8597] † תִּפְאָרָה **n.f.** beauty, glory;—´תּ [Is 28:5](https://ref.ly/Isa28:5), [Je 48:17](https://ref.ly/Jer48:17); elsewh. abs. and cstr. תִּפְאֶרֶת [Is 3:18](https://ref.ly/Isa3:18) \+ 20 t.; תִּפְאָרֶת [Pr 28:12](https://ref.ly/Prov28:12) \+ 6 t.; sf. תִּפְאַרְתִּי [Is 46:13](https://ref.ly/Isa46:13) \+ 20 t. sf.;—**1\.** *beauty, finery* [Is 3:18](https://ref.ly/Isa3:18); of garments [52:1](https://ref.ly/Isa52:1); jewels Ez [16:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:17), [16:39](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:39), [23:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:26), cf. [2 Ch 3:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:6); flock [Je 13:20](https://ref.ly/Jer13:20); a man [Is 44:13](https://ref.ly/Isa44:13); city of Samaria [28:1](https://ref.ly/Isa28:1), [28:4](https://ref.ly/Isa28:4); diadem v:[5](https://ref.ly/Isa28:5). **2\.** *glory:* **a.** of rank: apparel of h. p. [Ex 28:2](https://ref.ly/Exod28:2), [28:40](https://ref.ly/Exod28:40) (P); ´עֲטֶרֶת ת *crown of glory* [Pr 4:9](https://ref.ly/Prov4:9), [16:31](https://ref.ly/Prov16:31), [Is 62:3](https://ref.ly/Isa62:3), [Je 13:18](https://ref.ly/Jer13:18), Ez [16:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:12), [23:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:42); greatness of monarch [Est 1:4](https://ref.ly/Esth1:4); house of David and inhabitants of Jerus. [Zc 12:7](https://ref.ly/Zech12:7), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Zech12:7). **b.** of renown ´לְשֵׁם ולת [Dt 26:19](https://ref.ly/Deut26:19), [1 Ch 22:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:5), [Je 13:11](https://ref.ly/Jer13:11), [33:9](https://ref.ly/Jer33:9). **c.** attribute of ´י [Psalm 71:8](https://ref.ly/Ps71:8), [1 Ch 29:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:11); ´שֵׁם ת [Is 63:14](https://ref.ly/Isa63:14), [1 Ch 29:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:13); עֹז ´ת [Psalm 89:18](https://ref.ly/Ps89:18); ´זְרוֺעַ ת [Is 63:12](https://ref.ly/Isa63:12); hence ´ת in ´י's sanctuary [Psalm 96:6](https://ref.ly/Ps96:6), ´בֵּית תִּפְאַרְתִּ [Is 60:7](https://ref.ly/Isa60:7) cf. [63:15](https://ref.ly/Isa63:15) (of heavenly temple), [64:10](https://ref.ly/Isa64:10); ´ת as ´י's gift to Isr. [46:13](https://ref.ly/Isa46:13) cf. [60:19](https://ref.ly/Isa60:19) (also [Psalm 89:18](https://ref.ly/Ps89:18) supr.); of future fruit of land [4:2](https://ref.ly/Isa4:2); design. of ark of ´י [Psalm 78:61](https://ref.ly/Ps78:61). **3\. a.** *honour* of nation Isr. [La 2:1](https://ref.ly/Lam2:1). **b.** *glorying, boasting,* of individ., [Is 20:5](https://ref.ly/Isa20:5), [Pr 17:6](https://ref.ly/Prov17:6), [19:11](https://ref.ly/Prov19:11), [20:29](https://ref.ly/Prov20:29), [28:12](https://ref.ly/Prov28:12); warrior Ju [4:9](https://ref.ly/Judg4:9); monarchs [Is 10:12](https://ref.ly/Isa10:12); nations [Is 13:19](https://ref.ly/Isa13:19), Ez [24:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:25), ´מַקֵּל ת *rod (sceptre) of glorying* [Je 48:17](https://ref.ly/Jer48:17) (others **1**).

## II פאר (id: BDB07554)

II. פאר (doubtful √).

> \[Str6288] † \[פּאֹרָה] **n.f.** bough;—pl., all in fig.: of vine, abs. פּאֹרוֺת Ez [17:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:6) (Baer פֹּראֹות); of cedar, sf. פּאֹרֹתוֺ [31:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:5) (Kt; יו—ָ Qr); פּאֹרֹתָיו v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:6), פֹּראֹתָיו v:[8](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:8), v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:12), v:[13](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:13).

> \[Str6288,6333,6288] † פארה Kt., פּוּרָה Qr **n. f. coll.** boughs [Is 10:33](https://ref.ly/Isa10:33) (van d. H. Baer פֻּארָה).

> \[Str6286] † \[פֵּאֵר] **vb. denom. Pi.** go over the boughs;—*Impf.* 2 ms. תְּפָאֵר [Dt 24:20](https://ref.ly/Deut24:20) *thou shalt* not *go over the boughs* after thee (i.e. glean).

> \[Str6289] † פָּארוּר **n.\[m.]** mng. dub.; only ´קִבְּצוּ פ [Na 2:11](https://ref.ly/Nah2:11), [Jo 2:6](https://ref.ly/Joel2:6); Thes, all faces *gather a glow (glow* with dread, fr. assumed √ פאר), so We Now; AE Hi al. *gather in* (their) *beauty* (√ I. פאר; grow pale); Vrss AV *gather blackness* (fr. פָּרוּר *a pot !*), v. Dr; all very uncertain.

> \[Str6290] † פָּארָן **n.pr.loc.** Paran, Φαραν (cf. n.pr. פארן Sin. Inscr.);—usu. ´מִדְבַּר פ home of Ishmael [Gn 21:21](https://ref.ly/Gen21:21) (E), traversed by Isr. in Exodus [Nu 12:16](https://ref.ly/Num12:16) (E), [10:12](https://ref.ly/Num10:12), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Num13:2), [13:26](https://ref.ly/Num13:26) (all P); it lay NE. fr. (the traditional) Sinai, with Gulf of Akaba and the ï Arabah as its E. border; in the same region was ´הַר פ [Dt 33:2](https://ref.ly/Deut33:2), Hb [3:3](https://ref.ly/Hab3:3), —perh. coast\-range of mts. along W. shore of Gulf of Akaba; ´פ alone [1 K 11:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:18), [11:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:18) (betw. Midian and Egypt); [Dt 1:1](https://ref.ly/Deut1:1) (loc. dub.).—[1 S 25:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:1) v. II. מָעוֺן sub עון.—Vid. RobBR i, 177 f. PalmerDesert of Exodus, 284 ff., Di DrDt.

## פגג (id: BDB07560)

פגג (√ of foll.; mng. dub.; NH פַּגָּה *unripe fig,* pl. פַּגִּין; so Syr. ܦܰܓܳܐ in Lexx; Ar. فِجٌّ *unripe fruit;* Targum פַּגָּא *unripe grape;* cf. Post in HastingsDB. ‘ Fig ’ TristrNHB 352 Löwp. 391\).

> \[Str6291] † \[פַּגָּה] **n.f.** (cf. NH) **early fig;**—pl. sf. פַּגֶּיהָ Ct [2:13](https://ref.ly/Song2:13).

* **Associated Passages:** Song of Songs 2:13


## פגל (id: BDB07562)

פגל (√ of foll.; mng. dub.; Ar. فَجَلَ (فَجُلَ, فَجِلَ) is *be thick and soft, flaccid;* NH פִּגּוּל \= BH, cf. LevyNHWB s.v.).

> \[Str6292] † \[פִּגּוּל] **n.m.**:[Lv 7:18](https://ref.ly/Lev7:18) foul thing, refuse, but only as term. techn. of unclean sacrificial flesh;—it is ´פ if eaten on third day [Lv 7:18](https://ref.ly/Lev7:18) (P), [19:7](https://ref.ly/Lev19:7) (H); cf. ´בְּשַר פ Ez [4:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:14) (undefined); pl. מְרַק פִּגֻּלִים [Is 65:4](https://ref.ly/Isa65:4) *broth of refuse things* (Kt פרק), RS Sem i. 325, 2nd ed. 343 thinks of flesh with the blood; Duad loc. of mice and other unclean animals.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 65:4; Leviticus 19:7; Leviticus 7:18; Ezekiel 4:14

Pers. [1 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:5), [Ex 5:20](https://ref.ly/Exod5:20) (J), c. sf. Pers. [Am 5:19](https://ref.ly/Amos5:19); שׁוֺר ´פ [Ex 23:4](https://ref.ly/Exod23:4); c. ב Pers. [Gn 32:2](https://ref.ly/Gen32:2) (E), [Nu 35:19](https://ref.ly/Num35:19), [35:21](https://ref.ly/Num35:21) (P); c. ב loc. [Is 64:5](https://ref.ly/Isa64:5) (subj. ´י), so [47:3](https://ref.ly/Isa47:3) acc. to Ew De Di al.; CheHpt אֶפְרַע (reading, with Du, אמר for אָדָם). **3\.** *encounter* with hostility, *fall upon* c. ב Pers. [Je 27:18](https://ref.ly/Jer27:18), [Ru 1:16](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:16), \+ ל Pers. on whose behalf [Gn 23:8](https://ref.ly/Gen23:8) **5\.** *strike, touch*, of boundary, c. ב loc. [Je 15:11](https://ref.ly/Jer15:11) (otherwise Gie; Co del. v. as gloss). **3\.** *make entreaty*, 3 pl. הִפְגִּעוּ [Je 36:25](https://ref.ly/Jer36:25) c. ב Pers.; more gen. *interpose, Impf.* 3 ms. יַפְגִּיעַ [Is 53:12](https://ref.ly/Isa53:12), c. ל Pers. in whose behalf; *Pt.* מַפְגִּיעַ [59:16](https://ref.ly/Isa59:16) *one interposing*. **4\.** *make attack*, only *Pt.* [Jb 36:32](https://ref.ly/Job36:32) *assailant*, but Ol Bu Du al. מִפְגָּע *mark*.

\[Str6296] † \[פָּגַר] **vb. [Is 14:19](https://ref.ly/Isa14:19); usu. pl. [34:3](https://ref.ly/Isa34:3), [66:24](https://ref.ly/Isa66:24), [Je 31:40](https://ref.ly/Jer31:40), [33:5](https://ref.ly/Jer33:5), [41:9](https://ref.ly/Jer41:9), Ez [6:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:5), [43:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:7), [43:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:9), [Lv 26:30](https://ref.ly/Lev26:30), [Nu 14:29](https://ref.ly/Num14:29), [14:32](https://ref.ly/Num14:32), [14:33](https://ref.ly/Num14:33), [2 Ch 20:34](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:34), — v:[25](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:25) rd. prob. בְּגָדִים, so LXX (?), Vulgate Be Krochm Kau Kit;—פְּגָרִים מֵתִים [2 K 19:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:35) \= [Is 37:36](https://ref.ly/Isa37:36); sg. coll. ## פָּגַשׁ (id: BDB07570)

\[Str6298] † \[פָּגַשׁ] **vb.** meet, encounter (syn. פָּגַע);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 S. פָּ\+גשְׁתִּי [Gn 33:8](https://ref.ly/Gen33:8); 3 pl. consec. וּפָֽגְשׁוּ [Is 34:14](https://ref.ly/Isa34:14); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יִפְגַָֽשְְׁ֫ [Gn 32:18](https://ref.ly/Gen32:18) (Gi; Baer יִֽפְגָֽשְְׁ֫; van d. H. יִֽפְגָּֽשְְׁ֫), וַיִּפְגְּשֵׁהוּ [Ex 4:24](https://ref.ly/Exod4:24), [4:27](https://ref.ly/Exod4:27); 3 fs. וַתִּפְגַשׁ [1 S 25:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:20) (rd. prob. וַתִּפְגּשׁ Ges§ 47 k, cf. van d. H), etc.; *Inf. abs.* פָּגוֺש [Pr 17:12](https://ref.ly/Prov17:12); *cstr.* פְּגשׁ [Je 41:6](https://ref.ly/Jer41:6); —*meet*, c. acc. [Gn 33:8](https://ref.ly/Gen33:8), [1 S 25:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:20), [Is 34:14](https://ref.ly/Isa34:14), [Je 41:6](https://ref.ly/Jer41:6), so c. sf. [Gn 32:18](https://ref.ly/Gen32:18), [Ex 4:24](https://ref.ly/Exod4:24), [4:27](https://ref.ly/Exod4:27), [2 S 2:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:13); *meet, encounter*, of bear, c. ב Pers. [Pr 17:12](https://ref.ly/Prov17:12), of ´י (like a bear) [Ho 13:8](https://ref.ly/Hos13:8) (c. sf. Pers.). **Niph.** *meet together, each other, Pf.* 3 pl. נִפְגָּֽשׁוּ [Pr 22:2](https://ref.ly/Prov22:2), [29:13](https://ref.ly/Prov29:13), fig. of חֶסֶד וֶאֱמֶת [Psalm 85:11](https://ref.ly/Ps85:11). **Pi.** intens. *Impf.* 3 mpl. יְפַגְּשׁוּ חשֶׁךְ [Jb 5:14](https://ref.ly/Job5:14) *they keep encountering darkness* in the daytime.

## פָּדָה (id: BDB07571)

\[Str6299,6302] † פָּדָה **vb.** ransom (NH in deriv.; Ph. in n.pr.; As. *padû, id.,* Ar. فَدَى; Eth. ፈደየ፡; Min. פדית *redeemed* or *purchased land* MordtBeitr. 23\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. פָּדָה [Lv 27:27](https://ref.ly/Lev27:27) \+; sf. פָּֽדְ [Jb 5:20](https://ref.ly/Job5:20); 2 ms. פָּדִיתָ [Dt 9:26](https://ref.ly/Deut9:26) \+, etc.; *Impf.* יִפְדֶּה [Psalm 49:8](https://ref.ly/Ps49:8) \+, etc.; *Imv.* פְּדֵה [Psalm 25:22](https://ref.ly/Ps25:22); sf. פְּדֵנִי [Psalm 26:11](https://ref.ly/Ps26:11) \+ 3 t.; *Inf. abs.* פָּדֹה [Nu 18:15](https://ref.ly/Num18:15), [Psalm 49:8](https://ref.ly/Ps49:8); *cstr.* לִפְדּוֺת [2 S 7:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:23) \= [1 Ch 17:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:2); *Pt. act.* פּוֺדֶה [Psalm 34:23](https://ref.ly/Ps34:23); sf. פֹּדְ [Dt 13:6](https://ref.ly/Deut13:6); *pass.* pl. cstr. פְּדוּיֵּי [Is 35:10](https://ref.ly/Isa35:10), [51:11](https://ref.ly/Isa51:11), [Nu 3:49](https://ref.ly/Num3:49); also abs. פְּדוּיִם [Nu 3:51](https://ref.ly/Num3:51) (Qr; Kt פדים), cstr. פְּדוּיֵי v:[46](https://ref.ly/Num3:46); sf. פְּדוּיָו [Nu 18:16](https://ref.ly/Num18:16) (all n. abstr. acc. to Di Köii. 1, 138, v. פְּדוּיִם, פִּדְיֹם infr.);—*ransom:* **1\.** for an assessed price [Ex 13:13](https://ref.ly/Exod13:13), [13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15), [34:20](https://ref.ly/Exod34:20) (J), [Lv 27:27](https://ref.ly/Lev27:27), [Nu 3:46](https://ref.ly/Num3:46), [3:48](https://ref.ly/Num3:48), [3:49](https://ref.ly/Num3:49), [3:51](https://ref.ly/Num3:51), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Num18:15), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Num18:16), [18:17](https://ref.ly/Num18:17) (P). **2\.** fr. violence and death [1 S 14:45](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:45), [Jb 6:23](https://ref.ly/Job6:23), [Psalm 49:8](https://ref.ly/Ps49:8). **3\.** God subj. (underlying thought of payment): **a.** fr. Egypt, c. מִן, מִבֵּית עֲבָדִים [Dt 7:8](https://ref.ly/Deut7:8), [13:6](https://ref.ly/Deut13:6), [Mi 6:4](https://ref.ly/Mic6:4); מִנִּי־חָר [Psalm 78:42](https://ref.ly/Ps78:42); ממצרים [2 S 7:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:23) (but del. פָּדִיתָ GeiUrschr. 288 We Dr al.) \= [1 Ch 17:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:21); מִשָּׁם [Dt 24:18](https://ref.ly/Deut24:18); abs. [Dt 9:26](https://ref.ly/Deut9:26), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Deut15:15), [21:8](https://ref.ly/Deut21:8), [Ne 1:10](https://ref.ly/Neh1:10). **b.** from exile, [Je 31:11](https://ref.ly/Jer31:11), [Zc 10:8](https://ref.ly/Zech10:8); יְשֻׁבוּן ´פְּדוּיֵּי י [Is 35:10](https://ref.ly/Isa35:10) \= [51:11](https://ref.ly/Isa51:11). **c.** in gen. [Ho 7:13](https://ref.ly/Hos7:13); מִכֹּל צָרוֺתָיו [Psalm 25:22](https://ref.ly/Ps25:22); מִכֹּל עֲוֺנוֺתָיו [Psalm 130:8](https://ref.ly/Ps130:8). **d.** c. acc. individ. [Psalm 26:11](https://ref.ly/Ps26:11), [31:6](https://ref.ly/Ps31:6), [44:27](https://ref.ly/Ps44:27), [69:19](https://ref.ly/Ps69:19); Abraham [Is 29:22](https://ref.ly/Isa29:22); ´פ נפשׁ [Psalm 34:23](https://ref.ly/Ps34:23), [71:23](https://ref.ly/Ps71:23); \+ מכל צרה [2 S 4:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:9), [1 K 1:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:29); מֵעֲבֹר בַּשָּׁחַת [Jb 33:28](https://ref.ly/Job33:28); מִקְּרָב־לִי [Psalm 55:19](https://ref.ly/Ps55:19); מִמָּוֶת [Jb 5:20](https://ref.ly/Job5:20); מיד שׁאול [Psalm 49:16](https://ref.ly/Ps49:16), [Ho 13:14](https://ref.ly/Hos13:14); מֵעשֶׁק אָדָם [Psalm 119:134](https://ref.ly/Ps119:134); מִכַּף עָרִיצִים [Je 15:21](https://ref.ly/Jer15:21). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. (\+ **Hoph.** *Inf. abs.*) הָפְדֵּה לאֹ נִפְדָּתָה [Lv 19:20](https://ref.ly/Lev19:20) *she hath not been at all ransomed* (from bondage); *Impf.* יִפָּדֶה [Lv 27:29](https://ref.ly/Lev27:29) (from ban); 3 fs. צִיּוֺן בְּמִשְׁפָּט תִּפָּדֶָה [Is 1:27](https://ref.ly/Isa1:27). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. וְהֶפְדָּהּ [Ex 21:8](https://ref.ly/Exod21:8) *he shall let her be ransomed*. **Hiph.** *Inf. abs.* v. **Niph.**

[Psalm 111:9](https://ref.ly/Ps111:9), שֵמְתִּי פְדֻת בֵּין עַמִּי וּבֵין עַמְֶָּ [Ex 8:19](https://ref.ly/Exod8:19) *I will set a ransom* (distinguishing) *between my people and thy people* Targum, but improb. and text dub.; LXX SyrVer Vulgate *set a distinction* (rdg. what ?).

## פֶּה (id: BDB07583)

\[Str6310,6366,6374] פֶּה:485 **n.m.**:[Jos 9:2](https://ref.ly/Josh9:2) mouth (prob. bilit. \[פו, פי] Sta§ 183; NH \= BH; Ph. לפי *acc. to;* As. *pû, mouth;* Ar. فُوهٌ, فَاهٌ, فِيهٌ; Eth. እፍ፡; Syr. ܠܦܽܘܬ *acc. to;* but also Ar. فَمّ, فُمّ, فِمّ, BAram. פֻּם, Targum פּוּמָא; Syr. ܦܽܘܡܳܐ; Mand. פומא NöM § 87\);—abs. ´פ [Ex 4:10](https://ref.ly/Exod4:10) \+, cstr. פִּי [Is 1:20](https://ref.ly/Isa1:20) \+; sf. 1 S. פִּי [Gn 45:12](https://ref.ly/Gen45:12) \+, 2 ms. פִּיְ [41:40](https://ref.ly/Gen41:40) \+, 3 ms. פִּיו [25:28](https://ref.ly/Gen25:28) \+, פִּיהוּ [Ex 4:15](https://ref.ly/Exod4:15) \+, 3 mpl. פִּיהֶם Ju [7:6](https://ref.ly/Judg7:6) \+, פִּימוֺ [Psalm 17:10](https://ref.ly/Ps17:10) \+ 2 t., etc.; pl. פִּיּוֺת [Pr 5:4](https://ref.ly/Prov5:4), פֵיוֺת (שְׁנֵי) Ju [3:16](https://ref.ly/Judg3:16), פִּיפִיּוֺת [Is 41:15](https://ref.ly/Isa41:15), [Psalm 149:6](https://ref.ly/Ps149:6); פִּים [1 S 13:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:21) (corrupt, v. Dr al.);—**1\. a.** *mouth,* of man, organ of eating and drinking [Gn 25:28](https://ref.ly/Gen25:28), Ju [7:6](https://ref.ly/Judg7:6), [1 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:26), [14:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:27), [Ne 9:20](https://ref.ly/Neh9:20), [Pr 19:24](https://ref.ly/Prov19:24) \= [26:15](https://ref.ly/Prov26:15) \+; fig. as finding things sweet [Jb 20:12](https://ref.ly/Job20:12), [Psalm 119:103](https://ref.ly/Ps119:103); fig. of earth [Gn 4:11](https://ref.ly/Gen4:11), swallowing Korah, etc. [Nu 16:30](https://ref.ly/Num16:30), [16:32](https://ref.ly/Num16:32), [26:10](https://ref.ly/Num26:10), [Dt 11:6](https://ref.ly/Deut11:6); fig. also of She’ôl, וּפָֽעֲרָה פִיהָ [Is 5:14](https://ref.ly/Isa5:14), cf. Toy). **5\. a.** *extremity, end* פֶּה לָפֶה *from end to end,* of a temple [2 K 10:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:21); city [21:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:16), מִפֶּה אֶל־פֶּה of land [Ezr 9:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:11). **b.** \= portion, פִּי שְׁנַיִם † [Dt 21:17](https://ref.ly/Deut21:17), [2 K 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:9), [Zc 13:8](https://ref.ly/Zech13:8). **6\.** with preps.:—**a.** אֶל פִּי, v. infr. **d** (2\). **b.** כְּפִי: (*a*) *acc. to the command of,* [1 Ch 12:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:23); (*b*) *acc. to the mouth of,* i.e. *in proportion to* (cf. As. *ki\-i pi\-i,* DlHWB 325\), † [Ex 16:21](https://ref.ly/Exod16:21) they gathered it אִישׁ כְּפִי אָכְלוֺ each *in proportion to* his eating (v:[16](https://ref.ly/Exod16:16), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Exod16:18) לְפִי), [Lv 25:52](https://ref.ly/Lev25:52) כְּפִי שָׁנָיו *acc. to* the number of his years (cf. v:[16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16) לְפִי); [Nu 6:21](https://ref.ly/Num6:21), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Num7:5) אִישׁ כְּפִי עֲבֹדָתוֺ (so [2 Ch 31:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:2)), [7:7](https://ref.ly/Num7:7), [7:8](https://ref.ly/Num7:8), [35:8](https://ref.ly/Num35:8) (all P); [Jb 33:6](https://ref.ly/Job33:6) הֵן־אֲנִי כְפִיְ לָאֵל I am *in the proportion of* thee as regards God, i.e. I stand towards God even as thou dost. As **conj.,** כְּפִי אֲשֶׁר, † [Ml 2:9](https://ref.ly/Mal2:9) כְּפי אֲשֶׁר אֵינְכֶם שֹׁמְרִים אֶת־דְּרָכַי *acc. as*ye do not keep, etc.; and without אשׁר [Zc 2:4](https://ref.ly/Zech2:4) כְּפִי אִישׁ־לאֹ נָשָא ראֹשׁוֺ *in such proportion that* none did lift up his head (but We Now אֲשֶׁר for אִישׁ, that it, Judah, did not, etc.). **c.** לְפִי, nearly i.q. כְּפִי (v. לְ **5 i** *b*), but more common (cf. Aram. לְפוּם, ܠܦܽܘܬ): (1\) † [Gn 47:12](https://ref.ly/Gen47:12) לְפִי הַטָּף, [Ex 12:4](https://ref.ly/Exod12:4) ye shall compute אִישׁ לְפִי אָכְלוֺ each *acc. to* his eating (acc. to the quantity which his family will consume), [16:16](https://ref.ly/Exod16:16), [16:18](https://ref.ly/Exod16:18), [Lv 25:16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16) לְפִי רֹב הַשָּׁנִים, v:[16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16), v:[51](https://ref.ly/Lev25:51), [27:16](https://ref.ly/Lev27:16) לְפִי זַרְעוֺ, [Nu 26:54](https://ref.ly/Num26:54), [Jos 18:4](https://ref.ly/Josh18:4), [1 K 17:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:1) except לְפִי דְבָרִי, [Ho 10:12](https://ref.ly/Hos10:12) קִצְרוּ לְפִי חֶסֶד (‖לִצְדָקָה), [Pr 12:8](https://ref.ly/Prov12:8), [27:2](https://ref.ly/Prov27:2): sq. inf. [Nu 9:17](https://ref.ly/Num9:17) לְפִי הֵעָלוֺת הֶעָנָן \= *acc. as* the cloud was lifted up, [Je 29:10](https://ref.ly/Jer29:10). (2\) in the phr. לְפִי חֶרֶב *acc. to the mouth,* or *measure,* of the sword, i.e. as the sword devours, without quarter, [Gn 34:26](https://ref.ly/Gen34:26), [Ex 17:13](https://ref.ly/Exod17:13), [Nu 21:24](https://ref.ly/Num21:24) \+ oft. **d.** עַל פִּי: (1\) *acc. to* (עַל **1 f** *a*) *the mouth of,* i.e. (*a*) the command of, [Gn 41:40](https://ref.ly/Gen41:40), [45:21](https://ref.ly/Gen45:21), [Ex 17:1](https://ref.ly/Exod17:1), [38:21](https://ref.ly/Exod38:21), [Nu 3:16](https://ref.ly/Num3:16), [3:39](https://ref.ly/Num3:39) \+ (esp. P), [2 K 23:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:35); (β) the evidence or sentence of, [Dt 17:6](https://ref.ly/Deut17:6) עַל־פִּי עֵד אֶחָד, [19:15](https://ref.ly/Deut19:15), [21:5](https://ref.ly/Deut21:5) (on עַל־פִּי הַָ\+נּבֶל [Am 6:5](https://ref.ly/Amos6:5), v. **2 e** supr.). (*2*) *acc. to the measure of, in accordance with,* † [Gn 43:7](https://ref.ly/Gen43:7) we told him על פי הדברים האלה *acc. to* these words (i.e. the questions Joseph asked), [Ex 34:27](https://ref.ly/Exod34:27) *in accordance with* these words, [Lv 27:8](https://ref.ly/Lev27:8) על־פי אשׁר תַּשִּיג יַד הנֹּדֵר *acc. to* what the hand of the vower can reach to, v:[18](https://ref.ly/Lev27:18) (cf. לְפִי [25:16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16), [25:51](https://ref.ly/Lev25:51)), [Dt 17:10](https://ref.ly/Deut17:10), [17:11](https://ref.ly/Deut17:11), [2 S 13:32](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:32) (but v. שוּמָה), [Pr 22:6](https://ref.ly/Prov22:6) צֲנֹח לַנַּעַר על פי דרכו. In the same sense אֶל פִּי, † [Jos 15:13](https://ref.ly/Josh15:13), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Josh17:4), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Josh21:3).

## פֹּה (id: BDB07584)

\[Str6311] פֹּה:58, and (Ez 40–41, 23 out of 35 t.) פּוֺ, also † [Jb 38:11](https://ref.ly/Job38:11) b פֹּא, **adv. loc.** here, hither (prob. from the same demonst. √ found in ف *so, then* (oft. in apod.), אַף, אֵפוֺא: cf. Köii. l, 247 f., 243, 331 f.):—**1\.** *here:* **a.** [Gn 19:12](https://ref.ly/Gen19:12) עֹד מִי־לְךָ פֹּה whom hast thou still *here ?* [22:5](https://ref.ly/Gen22:5) שְׁבוּ לָכֶם פֹּה, [40:15](https://ref.ly/Gen40:15), [Nu 22:8](https://ref.ly/Num22:8), [32:16](https://ref.ly/Num32:16), [Dt 5:28](https://ref.ly/Deut5:28), [12:8](https://ref.ly/Deut12:8), Ju [4:20](https://ref.ly/Judg4:20) הֲיֵשׁ פֹּה אִישׁ, [2 K 3:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:11), etc.; asked in some surprise, Ju [18:3](https://ref.ly/Judg18:3) מַה־לְּךָ פֹה \= what is thy business *here ?* simil. † [1 K 19:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:9), [19:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:13), [Is 22:16](https://ref.ly/Isa22:16), [52:5](https://ref.ly/Isa52:5) וְעַתָּה מַה־לִּי־פֹה. Cf. the syn. בָּזֶה, בֹּה **2\. b.** עַד־פֹּה † [Jb 38:11](https://ref.ly/Job38:11) a. **c.** מִפֹּה or מִפּוֺ, only Ez 40–41, and always (exc. [40:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:12) a, where it must be supplied with LXX SyrVer) repeated, *on this side ... on that side* (cf. מִזֶּה …מִזֶּה), Ez [40:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:10), [40:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:10), [40:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:12) b, [b](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:12), [40:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:21), [40:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:21), etc. **d.** with the interrog. אֵי, אֵיפֹה *where ?* v. p. 33\. † **2\.** after a verb of motion, *hither,* [1 S 16:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:11) עַד־בּאֹוֺ פֹה, [Ezr 4:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:2).

## פּוּג (id: BDB07587)

\[Str6313] † \[פּוּג] **vb.** grow numb (Ar. فَاجَ *grow cool;* Syr. ܦܳܓ *be cold;* Targum פּוּג *cease, be helpless;* NH *id. vanish*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיָּפָג לִבּוֺ [Gn 45:26](https://ref.ly/Gen45:26); 3 fs. תָּפוּג [Psalm 77:3](https://ref.ly/Ps77:3) of hand (si vera l., v. נגר), i.e. drop helpless; Hb [1:4](https://ref.ly/Hab1:4) of תּוֺרָה, i.e. be ineffective. **Niph.** *Pf.* 1 s. נְפוּגֹתִי וְנִדְכֵּיתִי [Psalm 38:9](https://ref.ly/Ps38:9) *I am benumbed and crushed* (fig.); rd. prob. also אֶפּוֺגָה [Psalm 88:16](https://ref.ly/Ps88:16) (for MT ἅπ. λεγ. אָפוּנָה), Ol Hup Dy Gr Che אָפוּגָה.

806:a. \[פּוּג], 1\.2 f.: rd. ‘Syr. ܦܓ *become cold* ([Mt 24:12](https://ref.ly/Matt24:12)), *fail, become weak, inefficient;* Targum פּוּג *become cold, fail, be* or (Pa.) *make ineffectual;* NH *id., fail, vanish.’*

> \[Str6314] † פּוּגַת (Ges§ 80 f) **n.f.** benumbing (fig.), cessation;—לָךְ ´אַל־תִּתְּנִי פ [La 2:18](https://ref.ly/Lam2:18) *grant thyself no benumbing* (rd. prob. פּוּגֹת).

> \[Str2014] † \[הֲפוּגָה] **n.f.** id.;—pl. מֵאֵין הֲפֻגוֺת [La 3:49](https://ref.ly/Lam3:49) (of weeping; form very strange, rd. prob. פֻּגוֺת),

## פּוּחַ (id: BDB07590)

\[Str6315] † \[פּוּחַ] **vb.** breathe, blow (NH פּוּחַ *blow out;* Ar. (فوخ), فَاخَ \= Heb., so Aram. פּוּחַ, ܦܳܚ);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יָפוּחַ הַיּוֺם Ct [2:17](https://ref.ly/Song2:17), [4:6](https://ref.ly/Song4:6) until *the day breathes,* i.e. grows cool. **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יָפִיחַ [Psalm 10:5](https://ref.ly/Ps10:5) \+; יָפִחַ [Pr 14:25](https://ref.ly/Prov14:25); וְיָפֵחַ Hb [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hab2:3), etc.; *Imv.* fs. הָפִיחִי Ct [4:16](https://ref.ly/Song4:16); —**1\.** *cause* garden (acc.) *to exhale* (sc. odours) Ct [4:16](https://ref.ly/Song4:16). **2\. a.** *puff, snort,* against, ב Pers., [Psalm 10:5](https://ref.ly/Ps10:5). **b.** עַל Pers., Ez [21:36](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:31) with the fire of my wrath *will I blow against thee.* **c.** *excite, inflame* city [Pr 29:8](https://ref.ly/Prov29:8). **d.** c. לוֺ [Psalm 12:6](https://ref.ly/Ps12:6) *puff, \= pant for it* (so most; Thes Hi al. *against whom men puff;* Bae אֹפִּיעַ לוֺ *I will shine forth to him*); לַקֵּן Hb [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hab2:3) the vision *panteth* (hasteth) *towards the end* (so most). **3\.** *breathe out, utter,* כְּזָבִים [Pr 6:19](https://ref.ly/Prov6:19), [14:5](https://ref.ly/Prov14:5), [14:25](https://ref.ly/Prov14:25), [19:5](https://ref.ly/Prov19:5), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Prov19:9), אֱמוּנָה [12:17](https://ref.ly/Prov12:17).

## I פּוּץ (id: BDB07603)

\[Str6327] † I. \[פּוּץ] **vb.** be dispersed, scattered (NH \= BH; perh. akin to Ar. فَضَّ *break asunder, scatter*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יָפוּצוּ [Psalm 68:2](https://ref.ly/Ps68:2), יָפֻצוּ Ez [48:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:16) \+; 3 fpl. וַתְּפוּצֶינָה Ez [34:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:5) \+, וּתְפוּצֶינָה [Zc 13:7](https://ref.ly/Zech13:7), etc.; *Imv.* mpl. פֻּצוּ [1 S 14:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:34); *Pt.* *pass.* pl. sf. פּוּצַי [Zp 3:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:10) (v. infr.);—*be dispersed, disperse,* בָּעָם [1 S 14:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:34); *be scattered,* of enemies [1 S 11:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:11), [Nu 10:35](https://ref.ly/Num10:35), [Psalm 68:2](https://ref.ly/Ps68:2); of Isr. (among nations) under fig. of flock Ez [34:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:5), [34:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:5), [Zc 13:7](https://ref.ly/Zech13:7); בַּת־פּוּצַי [Zp 3:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:10) is prob. crpt., and v:[9](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:9), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:10) gloss v. SchwallyZAW x (1890\), 203 Now Da; of besieging army [2 S 20:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:22) (מֵעַל הָעִיר); people from (מִן) possessions Ez [46:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:18); men (from Babel) [Gn 11:4](https://ref.ly/Gen11:4) (עַל loc.). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נָפ֫וֺצָה [Je 10:21](https://ref.ly/Jer10:21), 3 pl. נָפֹ֫צוּ [Gn 10:18](https://ref.ly/Gen10:18) \+, 2 mpl. נְפֹצֹתֶם Ez [11:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:17) \+, etc.; *Pt.* f. נָפ֫וֺצֶת [2 S 18:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:8) Qr (\> Kt נפצות) cf. Dr al.; pl. נְפֹצִים [1 K 22:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:17), נְפוֺצִים [2 Ch 18:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:16); —**1\.** *be scattered,* of army [2 K 25:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:5) (מֵעַל Pers.) \= [Je 52:8](https://ref.ly/Jer52:8), [1 K 22:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:17) (אֶל loc.) \= [2 Ch 18:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:16) (עַל), cf. [Je 40:15](https://ref.ly/Jer40:15) (abs.); of dispersed Isr. c. ב loc. Ez [11:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:17), [20:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:34), [20:41](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:41), [28:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:25), as flock [Je 10:21](https://ref.ly/Jer10:21), Ez [34:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:6) (עַל loc.), v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:12) (שָׁם); Egyptians [29:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:13) (שָׁמָּה); of battle *scattered* over country by defeat [2 S 18:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:8). **2\.** *be spread abroad,* of peoples [Gn 10:18](https://ref.ly/Gen10:18).

[Je 48:44](https://ref.ly/Jer48:44), but קוֺל פְּחָדִים † [Jb 15:21](https://ref.ly/Job15:21) \= a sound of terrors; ´פ פִּתְאֹם [22:10](https://ref.ly/Job22:10), [Pr 3:25](https://ref.ly/Prov3:25). **3\.** as design. dei † יִצְחָק ´פ [Gn 31:42](https://ref.ly/Gen31:42) (‖אֱהֵֽי אַבְרָהָם), so v:[53](https://ref.ly/Gen31:53) (‖ *id.;* both E).

Ez [23:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:6), [23:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:12); of Babylon [Je 51:23](https://ref.ly/Jer51:23), [51:57](https://ref.ly/Jer51:57), and Bab. allies Ez [23:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:23); of Media [Je 51:28](https://ref.ly/Jer51:28); Pers. *governor* of עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר, i. e. in Pal., [Hg 1:1](https://ref.ly/Hag1:1), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Hag1:14), [2:2](https://ref.ly/Hag2:2), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Hag2:21), [Ne 2:7](https://ref.ly/Neh2:7), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Neh2:9) \+ 8 t.; Pers. *satraps* in gen. ## פָּחַז (id: BDB07631)

\[Str6348] † \[פָּחַז] **vb.** be wanton, reckless (NH *id.* (rare); Ar. فَخَزَ *be haughty, boastful, reckless;* Targum פְּחַז, Syr. ܦܚܶܙ *be lascivious,* in der. spec. and deriv.);—**Qal** *Pt.* אֲנָשִׁים רֵיקִים וּפֹחֲזִים Ju [9:4](https://ref.ly/Judg9:4); of prophets [Zp 3:4](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:4) *extravagant* (‖אַנְשֵׁי בֹּגְדוֺת).

> \[Str6349] † פַּ֫חַז **n.\[m.]** wantonness, recklessness, unbridled license;—[Gn 49:4](https://ref.ly/Gen49:4), abstr. for concr. (\+ כַּמַּיִם *like* \[boiling, or overflowing] *water*).

> \[Str6350] † \[פַּחֲזוּת] **n.f.** recklessness, extravagance;—sf. תָם\- [Je 23:32](https://ref.ly/Jer23:32) of prophets.

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 49:4; Zephaniah 3:4; Judges 9:4; Jeremiah 23:32


## פחח (id: BDB07634)

פחח (√ of foll.; meaning dub.; cf. Aram. פָּחָא, ܦܰܚܳܐ, whence (Frä119) Ar. فَخٌّ as loan\-word).

> \[Str6341,6352] † I. פַּח **n.m.**:[Psalm 124:7](https://ref.ly/Ps124:7) bird\-trap (cf. WilkinsonEgyptians (1878\) ii, 103, 109 f. Dr:[Am 3:5](https://ref.ly/Amos3:5) HoffmZAW iii (1883\), 101\);—´פ abs. [Ho 5:1](https://ref.ly/Hos5:1) \+, פָּךְ [Je 48:44](https://ref.ly/Jer48:44) \+; cstr. פַּךְ [Ho 9:8](https://ref.ly/Hos9:8) \+; pl. פַּחִים [Je 18:22](https://ref.ly/Jer18:22) \+;—**1\.** lit. ´יַעֲלֶה פ [Am 3:5](https://ref.ly/Amos3:5) b (as metaph.; va del. ´פ), [Pr 7:23](https://ref.ly/Prov7:23), [Ec 9:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:12) (c. אָחוּז; both in sim.). **2\.** usually fig. (sts. ‖מוֺקֵשׁ, רֶשֶׁת, צַמִּים): **a.** of calamities and plots, יָקוֺשׁ ´פ [Ho 9:8](https://ref.ly/Hos9:8), cf. [Psalm 91:3](https://ref.ly/Ps91:3), [124:7](https://ref.ly/Ps124:7); ´פ alone [Je 48:43](https://ref.ly/Jer48:43) \= [Is 24:17](https://ref.ly/Isa24:17), [Jb 22:10](https://ref.ly/Job22:10), [Psalm 124:7](https://ref.ly/Ps124:7), [Pr 22:5](https://ref.ly/Prov22:5); לְ ´טָמַן פ [Je 18:22](https://ref.ly/Jer18:22) [Psalm 140:6](https://ref.ly/Ps140:6), [142:4](https://ref.ly/Ps142:4); לְ ´נָחַן פ [119:110](https://ref.ly/Ps119:110), לְ ´יָקַשׁ פ [141:9](https://ref.ly/Ps141:9); ´יאֹחֵז בְּעָקֵב פ [Jb 18:9](https://ref.ly/Job18:9); ´יִלָּכֵד בֵּפּ [Je 48:44](https://ref.ly/Jer48:44) \= [Is 24:18](https://ref.ly/Isa24:18). **b.** \= source or agent of calamity [Ho 5:1](https://ref.ly/Hos5:1), [Jos 23:13](https://ref.ly/Josh23:13) (D), [Is 8:14](https://ref.ly/Isa8:14), [Psalm 69:23](https://ref.ly/Ps69:23).—[Psalm 11:6](https://ref.ly/Ps11:6) v. פֶּחָם. below

> \[Str6351] † \[פָּחַח] **vb. Hiph. denom.** ensnare; *Inf. abs.* הָפֵחַ כֻּלָּם [Is 42:22](https://ref.ly/Isa42:22), c. בְּ loc. *an ensnaring* \[i. e. men have ensnared] *them all in*, etc.

> \[Str6341] † II. \[פַּח] **n.\[m.]** plate of metal; pl. דִקֻּעֵי פַחִים [Nu 17:3](https://ref.ly/Num17:3); cstr. וַיְרַקְּעוּ אֶת־פַּחֵי הַזָּהָב [Ex 39:3](https://ref.ly/Exod39:3).

## פחם (id: BDB07638)

## פָּטַר (id: BDB07646)

\[Str6362,6358,6359] † פָּטַר **vb.** separate, remove, set free (NH *depart, set free*, Niph. also *die;* Ph. פטר CIS102 a, 2 *depart (die ?)*; As. *paṭâru, split, break through, ipṭiru, ransom*, Tel Am. *escape, set free* (perh. Canaanit.); Ar.فَطَرَ *cleave, split*, also *create;* Eth. ፈጠረ፡ *create, fabricate;* Aram. פְּטַר, ܦܛܰܪ *withdraw, depart)*;—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´פ [2 Ch 23:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:8); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּפְטַר [1 S 19:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:10); *Pt. act.* פּוֺטֵר [Pr 17:14](https://ref.ly/Prov17:14); *pass.* פִּטוּרִים [1 Ch 9:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:33) Qr (Kt פטירים); cstr. פְּטוּרֵי [1 K 6:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:18), [6:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:29), [6:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:32), פְּטֻרֵי v:[35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:35); —**1\.** intrans. *remove* oneself, specif. *escape* [1 S 19:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:10) (מִפְּנֵי, cf. Dr). **2\.** trans. *set free* fr. duty [2 Ch 23:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:8) (acc. Pers.), so pass. [1 Ch 9:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:33) Qr (Kt פטירים adj. or Aram. pt., same mng.); יִם ´פ [Pr 17:14](https://ref.ly/Prov17:14) *set free, let out;* pass. פְּטוּרֵי צִצִּים term. techn. of ornament in temple, dub., usually *out\-spread* (garlands) *of flowers* [1 K 6:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:18), [6:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:29), [6:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:32), [6:35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:35). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יַפְטִירוּ בְשָפָה [Psalm 22:8](https://ref.ly/Ps22:8) *they separte with the lip*, i. e. open mouth wide (insultingly).

809:b. פָטַר **Hiph.**: cf. in Syr. Psalm3093.

> \[Str6363] † פֶּ֫טֶר **n.\[m.]** that which separates, first opens;—cstr., רֶחֶם ´פּ i. e. firstborn, of man and beast, Ez [20:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:26), [Ex 13:12](https://ref.ly/Exod13:12), [13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15), [34:19](https://ref.ly/Exod34:19), [Nu 18:15](https://ref.ly/Num18:15); appos. בְּכוֺר [Ex 13:2](https://ref.ly/Exod13:2), [Nu 3:12](https://ref.ly/Num3:12); רֶחֶם om. [Ex 13:12](https://ref.ly/Exod13:12), [13:13](https://ref.ly/Exod13:13), [34:19](https://ref.ly/Exod34:19), [34:20](https://ref.ly/Exod34:20).

[8:18](https://ref.ly/Exod8:18); *Impf.* 3 ms. יַפְלֶה [11:7](https://ref.ly/Exod11:7); *Imv.* ms. הַפְלֵה [Psalm 17:7](https://ref.ly/Ps17:7); —of ´י, *make separate,* c. acc. [31:22](https://ref.ly/Ps31:22)); *distinguish* between, בֵּין rei, [Ex 9:4](https://ref.ly/Exod9:4), בֵּין Pers. [11:7](https://ref.ly/Exod11:7).

> \[Str6423,6422] † I. פְּלֽנִי **pron.** a certain one; Gk. ὁ, ἡ δεῖνα (acc. to Thes from above √, through an unused noun פָּלֽן, prop. *one defined, a particular one:* NH *id.;* Aram. ܦܠܢ, f. ܦܠܢܝܬܐ; Arab. فُلَانٌ WAG i.§ 353 R.c, voc. فُلُ SiegfZW Th xxvii. 355; As *pulpul* HptBAS i. 114\), always joined with צַלְמֹנִי (prop. *one not mentioned*) in the sense of ‘ ein *gewisser* verschwiegener ’ (Ew§ 106 c, 2 Sta§ 150 b), \&ls *a certain* unnamed *one* ’ , i.e. *such a one;* [Ru 4:1](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:1) שְׁבָה־פֹּה פְּלֽנִי אַלְמֹנִי sit down here, *such a one* (Gk. ὦ οὗτος; Ar. يَاء فُلُ); in the phr. ´א ´מְקוֺם פ the place of *such a one* \= *such and such* a place, [1 S 21:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:3), [2 K 6:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:8). ## פלך (id: BDB07709)

פלך (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. فَلَكَ II. *be round* (esp. *hemispherical*), فَلْكَةٌ *whirl* of spindle, so As. *pilakku;* NH \= BH; Targum פֶּ לֶךְ *circuit, district;* As. also *pilku, district*).

Dr), [Pr 31:19](https://ref.ly/Prov31:19) (‖כִּישׁוֺר). **2\.** *district,* [Ne 3:9](https://ref.ly/Neh3:9), [3:12](https://ref.ly/Neh3:12), [3:14](https://ref.ly/Neh3:14), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Neh3:15), [3:16](https://ref.ly/Neh3:16), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Neh3:17), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Neh3:17), [3:18](https://ref.ly/Neh3:18).

## פָּלַץ (id: BDB07726)

\[Str6426] † \[פָּלַץ] **vb. Hithp.** shudder;—*Impf.* 3 mpl. יִתְפַּלָּחוּן [Jb 9:6](https://ref.ly/Job9:6) its (earth's) pillars *shudder.*

> \[Str6427] † פַּלָּחוּת **n.f.** shuddering;—´פ abs. [Jb 21:6](https://ref.ly/Job21:6), [Is 21:4](https://ref.ly/Isa21:4), Ez [7:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:18), [Psalm 55:6](https://ref.ly/Ps55:6).

> \[Str4656] † מִפְלֶ֫צֶת **n.f.** horrid thing (thing *to shudder at*);—לַאֲשֵׁרָה ´עָֽשְתָה מ [1 K 15:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:13) (of some abominable object of idolatry, not precisely known), so sf. מִפְלַצְתָּהּ v:[13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:13) \= [2 Ch 15:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:16), [15:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:16).

> \[Str8606] † \[תִּפְלֶ֫צֶת] **n.f.** shuddering, horror;—sf. תִּפְלַצְתְּ [Je 49:16](https://ref.ly/Jer49:16) *horror at thee!* (exclam.); not quite certain, v. Gie.

> \[Str6424] † פלש v. פלס. above

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 49:16; Job 9:6; Isaiah 21:4; Job 21:6; Ezekiel 7:18; 2 Chronicles 15:16; Psalms 55:6; 1 Kings 15:13
* **Associated Articles:** פלס (ID: `BDB07722@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## פָּלַשׁ (id: BDB07731)

\[Str6428] † \[פָּלַשׁ] **vb. Hithp.** act of mourning, poss. roll in (dust, etc. ; weakened fr. *burrow into*) (NH Pi. *break open, or through;* As. *palâšu, dig a hole;* Syr. ܦܠܰܫ *dig* or *break through;* but connexion dub., Vrss mostly *be\-sprinkle oneself*, Aq *roll oneself*);—*Pf.* I s. עָפָר הִתְפַּלָּשְׁתִּי [Mi 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mic1:10) Kt (Qr *Imv.* פַּלָּשִׁי\-), others mpl. פַּלָּשׁזּ\-); *Impf.* 3 mpl. בָּאֵפֶר יִתְפַּלָּשׁוּ Ez [27:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:30), so *Imv.* fs. הִתֵפַּלֵּשִׁי בָאֵפֶר [Je 6:26](https://ref.ly/Jer6:26) (cf.[Mi 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mic1:10) Qr); abs., mpl. הִתֵפַּלְּשׁוּ [25:34](https://ref.ly/Jer25:34).

> \[Str6429] † פְּלֶ֫שֶׁת **n.pr.terr.** Philistia, poet. and late;—פְּלָשֶׁת (ישְׁבֵי) [Ex 15:14](https://ref.ly/Exod15:14) (Φυλιστιειμ̀ elsewh. (οί) ἀλλόφυλοι), [Jo 4:4](https://ref.ly/Joel4:4), elsewhere פְּלֶשֶׁת [Is 14:29](https://ref.ly/Isa14:29), [14:31](https://ref.ly/Isa14:31), [Psalm 60:10](https://ref.ly/Ps60:10) \= [108:10](https://ref.ly/Ps108:10), [83:8](https://ref.ly/Ps83:8), [87:4](https://ref.ly/Ps87:4); As. *Palastu, Pilistu,* COT:[Gn 10:14](https://ref.ly/Gen10:14) Dlpa 288 f. Gk. Παλαιστίνη Herodii. 104, 106, vii. 89 JosAnt. i. 6\. 2 (also Φυλιστίνου); v. RelPal. 73 f.

> \[Str6430] פְּלִשְׁתִּי:288 **adj.gent.** Philistine, (οί) Φυλιστιειμ; and (not in Hex) (ό) ἀλλόφυλος, (οί) ἀλλόφυλοι;—only as subst.: ´הַפּ *the Philistine* (Goliath) slain by David [1 S 17:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:8) \+ 27 t. 17, [18:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:6), [19:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:5), [21:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:10), [22:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:10); one slain byAbishai [2 S 21:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:17); elsewhere pl. *the Philistines,*:255 usu. פְּלִשְׁתִּים without art., rarely ´הַפּח [1 S 4:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:7), [7:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:13) \+ (esp. c. prep. ´בַּפּ v:[13](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:13) \+, and then perh. due to punctuators), [Gn 10:14](https://ref.ly/Gen10:14) \= [1 Ch 1:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:12), [Gn 26:14](https://ref.ly/Gen26:14), [26:15](https://ref.ly/Gen26:15), [26:18](https://ref.ly/Gen26:18), Ju [3:31](https://ref.ly/Judg3:31) \+ 33 t. Ju, [1 S 4:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:1), [4:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:1) \+ 118 t. 1 S, [2 S 1:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:20) \+ 28 t. 2 S, \+; ´אֶרֶץ פ [Gn 21:32](https://ref.ly/Gen21:32), [21:34](https://ref.ly/Gen21:34) \+ 12 t.; ´שְדֵה פ [1 S 6:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:1), [27:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:7), [27:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:11); ´מֶלֶח פ [Gn 26:1](https://ref.ly/Gen26:1), [26:8](https://ref.ly/Gen26:8); ´שָרֵי פ [1 S 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:30) \+ 5 t.; ´סַרְנֵי פ [Jos 13:3](https://ref.ly/Josh13:3), Ju [3:3](https://ref.ly/Judg3:3) \+ 13 t.; ´יָם פ [Ex 23:31](https://ref.ly/Exod23:31); ´אֱלֽהֵי פ Ju [10:6](https://ref.ly/Judg10:6).—Cf. כְּרֵתִי, II. כַּפְתּוֺר. On Philistines v. also WMMAs.u. Eur.387 f. EvansCretan Pictographs, 100 ff. Schwally: ZWT xxxiv.103 f., 255\.

> \[Str6431] † פֶּ֫לֶת **n.pr.m. 1\.** Reubenite [Nu 16:1](https://ref.ly/Num16:1), Φαλεθ\[κ]; rd. prob. פַּלּוּא (q.v.) so Gf Dr al. **2\.** Judahite [1 Ch 2:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:33): Θαλεθ, Φαλεθ\[ατ].

> \[Str6432] † פְּלֵתִי **adj.gent.** from unknown n.pr.; only c. art. as n.coll. with הַכְּרֵתִי (q.v.) of David's guard [2 S 8:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:18), [15:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:18), [20:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:7), [20:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:23), [1 K 1:38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:38), [1:44](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:44), [1 Ch 18:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:17).

> \[Str6435] פֶּ־ן:133 (alw. with Makkeph) **conj.** (averting, or deprecating), lest (origin dub.: Thes Köii. 334 from פָּנָה , prop. subst. cstr. in accus. *for the aversion of;* but פָנָה is intrans.: NöM. 474 cps. the enclitic particle פּוֺן in Targum, *then a\[n*, ‘etwa,’ thinking that a part. with the mng. ‘es möchte etwa,’ spoken in a tone of alarm, might readily acquire a deprecatory force);—*lest:* **1\.** with *impf.* [Gn 3:3](https://ref.ly/Gen3:3) ye shall not eat thereof…פֶּן־תְּמֻתוּן *lest* ye die, [11:4](https://ref.ly/Gen11:4) let us build a city פֶּן־נָפוּץ *lest* we be scattered, [19:15](https://ref.ly/Gen19:15), [19:17](https://ref.ly/Gen19:17), [19:19](https://ref.ly/Gen19:19), [38:23](https://ref.ly/Gen38:23), [45:11](https://ref.ly/Gen45:11), [Ex 1:10](https://ref.ly/Exod1:10), [23:29](https://ref.ly/Exod23:29), [23:33](https://ref.ly/Exod23:33) etc., [2 K 10:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:23) (sq. יֵשׁ), [Is 6:10](https://ref.ly/Isa6:10), [27:3](https://ref.ly/Isa27:3), [28:22](https://ref.ly/Isa28:22), [48:5](https://ref.ly/Isa48:5), [48:7](https://ref.ly/Isa48:7), [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12), [7:3](https://ref.ly/Ps7:3) etc.; oft. after הִשָׁמֶר־לְךָ *take heed to thyself* [Gn 24:6](https://ref.ly/Gen24:6), [31:24](https://ref.ly/Gen31:24), and esp. in Dt, as [4:9](https://ref.ly/Deut4:9), [4:23](https://ref.ly/Deut4:23), [6:12](https://ref.ly/Deut6:12), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Deut8:11) al.; after a vb. of fearing [Gn 32:12](https://ref.ly/Gen32:12), and once, even, of swearing Ju [15:12](https://ref.ly/Judg15:12); deprecating pathetically, [Gn 44:34](https://ref.ly/Gen44:34) how shall I go up, etc.? *lest* I look upon the evil that will befal my father. Once, unusually, preceding the principal clause, [Pr 5:6](https://ref.ly/Prov5:6) אֹרַח חַיִּים פֶּן־תְּפַלֵּס *lest* she should make level the path of life, her ways are unstable, etc. (Ew Be Now; v. also Toy). Note esp. **a.** the idiom פֶּ־ן (אָמַרְתִּי) כִּי אָמַר *for he* (I) *said, Lest...,* implying always that some precaution has been taken to avert the dreaded contingency, † [Gn 26:9](https://ref.ly/Gen26:9) for I said, Lest I die on account of her (to obviate which, Isaac had called Rebecca his sister), [31:31](https://ref.ly/Gen31:31), [38:11](https://ref.ly/Gen38:11), [42:4](https://ref.ly/Gen42:4), [Ex 13:17](https://ref.ly/Exod13:17), [Nu 16:34](https://ref.ly/Num16:34), [1 S 13:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:19), [27:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:11) (לֵאמֹר), [Psalm 38:17](https://ref.ly/Ps38:17); with כי אמר implied [Gn 26:7](https://ref.ly/Gen26:7); cf., with an aposiop., [3:22](https://ref.ly/Gen3:22) and now, *lest* he put forth his hand, etc. (implying that measures are taken to prevent this, see v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen3:23)): cf. [Tob 8:9](https://ref.ly/Tob8:9). **b.** פֶּ־ן at the beg. of a sentence, with a dissuasive force, (Beware) *lest:* \+ [Is 36:18](https://ref.ly/Isa36:18) פֶּן־יַסִּית אֶתְכֶם חזקיהו (beware) *lest* Hezekiah deceive you, [Dt 29:17](https://ref.ly/Deut29:17), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Deut29:17) (sq. יֵשׁ), [Je 51:46](https://ref.ly/Jer51:46), [Jb 32:13](https://ref.ly/Job32:13), [36:18](https://ref.ly/Job36:18). **2\.** with *Pf.,* the result feared being conceived as having possibly already taken place; † [2 S 20:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:6) pursue after him, וְהִצִּיל …פֶּן־מָצָא לוֺ *lest he have found* him fenced cities, etc. (but the tense of והציל makes יִמְצָא prob., DrSm Bu Köiii. 486\), [2 K 2:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:16) ´וַיַּשְׁלִכֵהוּ וגו ´פֶּן־נְשָאוֺ רוּחַ י.
> 
> 814:b. פֶּ־ן 1\. I: after ‘Makkeph’ ins. ‘exc. [Gn 38:23](https://ref.ly/Gen38:23), [44:34](https://ref.ly/Gen44:34), [Dt 7:25](https://ref.ly/Deut7:25), [32:27](https://ref.ly/Deut32:27) (not Gi], [Is 27:3](https://ref.ly/Isa27:3), [Pr 25:8](https://ref.ly/Prov25:8): MandelkernConc. s.v..’

> \[Str6436] † פַּנַּג **n.\[m.]** unknown word, appar. some kind of food; ´וּדְבַשׁ וגו ´חטֵּי מִנִּית וּפ Ez [27:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:17); Vrss conject.; Co prop. וְדוֺנַג, so HoffmPh. Inschr. 15 Krae Berthol (‘ perhaps ’).

## פָּנָה (id: BDB07738)

\[Str6437] פָּנָה:135 **vb.** turn (NH *id.;* Syr. ܦܢܳܐ, Targum פְּנָא; Eth. ፈነወ፡: Ar. فَنِى *pass away, banish;* cf. Sab. פנה (variously) e.g. CISiv. Nos. 1, 37, 40; As.*pânu, face,* Ph. פנם, MI13, 18 לפני, sf. מפני l19);—**Qal**:117 *Pf.* 3 ms. ´פ [Je 6:4](https://ref.ly/Jer6:4) \+, 2 ms. פָּנִיתָ [2 S 9:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:8), etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִפְנֶה [1 S 13:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:17) \+, וַיִּ֫פֶן [Ex 2:12](https://ref.ly/Exod2:12) \+, 3 fs. וַתֵּ֫פֶן [1 K 10:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:13), 1 s. וָאֵ֫פֶן [Dt 9:15](https://ref.ly/Deut9:15), [10:5](https://ref.ly/Deut10:5), 1 pl. וַנֵּ֫פֶן [2:1](https://ref.ly/Deut2:1) \+, etc.; *Imv.* ms. פְּנֵה [Psalm 25:16](https://ref.ly/Ps25:16) \+, etc.; *Inf.abs.* פָּנֹה [Hg 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hag1:9); cstr. לִפְנוֺת [Gn 24:63](https://ref.ly/Gen24:63) \+, etc.; *Pt.* פֹּנֶה [Dt 29:17](https://ref.ly/Deut29:17) \+, etc.;—**1\.** † **a.** *turn* toward, אֶל Pers., lit., Ju [6:14](https://ref.ly/Judg6:14), [Is 13:14](https://ref.ly/Isa13:14) \= [Je 50:16](https://ref.ly/Jer50:16), [Jb 21:5](https://ref.ly/Job21:5) (i.e. attentively); cf. of branches Ez [17:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:6) (in allegory); c. עַל of direction [Gn 24:49](https://ref.ly/Gen24:49); fig. c. ´אֶל־י (in worship), [Is 45:22](https://ref.ly/Isa45:22), other gods [Ho 3:1](https://ref.ly/Hos3:1), [Dt 31:18](https://ref.ly/Deut31:18), [31:20](https://ref.ly/Deut31:20), [Lv 19:4](https://ref.ly/Lev19:4); for help [Lv 19:31](https://ref.ly/Lev19:31), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Lev20:6), [Jb 5:1](https://ref.ly/Job5:1); אֶל־אָוֶן [36:21](https://ref.ly/Job36:21); c. acc. הַמָּקוֺם אֲשֶׁר יִפְנֶה Ez [10:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:11); c. ה loc.[1 K 17:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:3), [Dt 2:3](https://ref.ly/Deut2:3) (\+ לָכֶם dat. eth.), Ct [6:1](https://ref.ly/Song6:1); c. דֶּרֶח [1 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:18), [13:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:18), [Jb 24:18](https://ref.ly/Job24:18), ´אֶל־ד Ju [20:42](https://ref.ly/Judg20:42), [1 S 13:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:17); fig. לְדֶרֶח [Is 53:6](https://ref.ly/Isa53:6), [56:11](https://ref.ly/Isa56:11); c. acc. כֹּל אֲשֶׁר־יִפְנֶה [1 S 14:47](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:47), so, \+ שָׁם, fig. [1 K 2:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:3), i.e. whatever thou undertakest, אֶל־כָּל־אֲשֶׁר יִפְנֶה [Pr 17:8](https://ref.ly/Prov17:8). † **b.** *turn* from, מִן loc. [Gn 18:22](https://ref.ly/Gen18:22), fig., of heart, c. ´מֵעִם י [Dt 29:17](https://ref.ly/Deut29:17), abs. \= *turn away* [30:17](https://ref.ly/Deut30:17); in phr. עֹרֶף ´פ *turn* (with) *the back* (cf. ראה עֹרֶף) [Je 18:17](https://ref.ly/Jer18:17)), \+ לִפְנֵי, [Jos 7:12](https://ref.ly/Josh7:12) (of flight), fig., \+ ´אֶל־י, [Je 2:27](https://ref.ly/Jer2:27), [32:33](https://ref.ly/Jer32:33). **c.** *turn* and do a thing, lit. [Nu 12:10](https://ref.ly/Num12:10), [2 Ch 20:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:24), [26:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:20), וַיִּפֶן בֹּה וָכֹה Ex 2:l2; c. לְמָעְלָה [Is 8:21](https://ref.ly/Isa8:21) abs. *look* (back) [2 Ch 13:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:14); c. אַתֲרִי *look behind one* (and see, or say), [Jos 8:20](https://ref.ly/Josh8:20), Ju [20:40](https://ref.ly/Judg20:40), [2 S 1:7](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:7), [2:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:20), [2 K 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:24), *look after* anotber Ez [29:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:16) (fig. for seek alliance with); c. אֶל of direction [Ex 16:10](https://ref.ly/Exod16:10), [Nu 17:7](https://ref.ly/Num17:7). **b.** of inanimate things, *face*, c. אֶל loc. c. יָצָא \= *to confront* [2 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:2).**b.** *to the front of,* after a vb. of motion, [Lv 6:7](https://ref.ly/Lev6:7) הַמִּזְבֵּחַ ´אֶל־פְּנֵ …הַקְרֵב אֹתָהּ, [9:5](https://ref.ly/Lev9:5), [16:2](https://ref.ly/Lev16:2), [Nu 17:8](https://ref.ly/Num17:8), [20:10](https://ref.ly/Num20:10). **c.** *towards* or *on the front of* (אֶל \= עַל: see p. 41\), Ez [41:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:4), [41:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:12), [41:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:15), [41:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:25), [42:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:2), [42:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:3), [42:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:7), [42:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:10), [42:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:13), [45:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:7), [48:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:21). **d.** *on to the surface of,* [Lv 14:53](https://ref.ly/Lev14:53) אֶל־פְּנֵי הַשָּדֶה …וְשִׁלַּח, Ez [16:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:5). † **2\.** אֶת־פְּנֵי *close by* (II. אֵת) *the face* or *front of:* hence **a.** *the presence of*, [1 S 2:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:11) עֵלִי ´אֶת־פ [1 K 12:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:6) who stood שְׁלֽמֹה ´אֶת־פ (cf. לִפְנֵי [10:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:8)), [Est 1:10](https://ref.ly/Esth1:10), [Pr 17:24](https://ref.ly/Prov17:24); pregn. (nisi וַיַּנִּחֵם leg., SyrVer Targum We Dr al.) [1 S 22:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:4). Spec. ´אֶת־פְּנֵי י [Gn 19:13](https://ref.ly/Gen19:13) ([1 S 2:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:17)), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Gen19:27), [1 S 2:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:18); and with נִרְאָה of the appearance of all males at the three annual חַגִּים, [Ex 34:23](https://ref.ly/Exod34:23) הָאָדֹן ´יֵרָאֶה כָּל־זְכוּרְ אֶת־פ, v:[24](https://ref.ly/Exod34:24), [Dt 16:16](https://ref.ly/Deut16:16) (cf. Dr), [31:11](https://ref.ly/Deut31:11), [1 S 1:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:22) (‖´י ´אל־פ [Ex 23:17](https://ref.ly/Exod23:17)); so פָּנַי alone [Ex 23:15](https://ref.ly/Exod23:15) \= [34:20](https://ref.ly/Exod34:20) (לאֹ יֵרָאוּ פָנַי רֵיקָם), [Is 1:22](https://ref.ly/Isa1:22), פָנֶיְ [Psalm 42:3](https://ref.ly/Ps42:3) (acc. to many, GesThes Di:[Ex 23:15](https://ref.ly/Exod23:15), Che:[Is 1:12](https://ref.ly/Isa1:12), crit. n., the vb. in all these passages was originally **Qal**, afterwards pointed as **Niph.** to avoid the expression *see the face of* ´י); אֶת־פָּנֶיְ *in thy presence* [Psalm 16:10](https://ref.ly/Ps16:10), [21:7](https://ref.ly/Ps21:7), [140:14](https://ref.ly/Ps140:13). **b.** *in front of,* [Gn 33:18](https://ref.ly/Gen33:18). הָעִיר ´וַיִּחַן אֶת־פ, [Lv 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lev4:6) פָרֹכֶת הַקֹּדֶשׁ ´אֶת־פ ´לִפְנֵי י v:[17](https://ref.ly/Lev4:17).—מֵאֵת פְּנֵי *from before,* v. II. אֵת **4 a. 3\.** בִּפְנֵי: † *(a) in the face of,* mostly in partic. phrases, with hostile import: thus, with יָרַק *to spit* [Nu 12:14](https://ref.ly/Num12:14), [Dt 25:9](https://ref.ly/Deut25:9); התיצב *to make a stand* [Dt 7:24](https://ref.ly/Deut7:24), [11:25](https://ref.ly/Deut11:25), and עמד [Jos 10:8](https://ref.ly/Josh10:8), לאֹ יַעֲמֹד אִישׁ בְּפָנֶיְ [21:42](https://ref.ly/Josh21:42), [23:9](https://ref.ly/Josh23:9); עָנָה *to answer* (give evidence) *against*, [Ho 5:5](https://ref.ly/Hos5:5) וענה גאון ישראל בפניו, [7:10](https://ref.ly/Hos7:10), [Jb 16:8](https://ref.ly/Job16:8); וְנָקֹטוּ בִּפְנֵיהֶם \= feel loathing *against* their own selves, Ez [6:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:9), so [20:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:43), [36:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:31). † *(b) in front of,* Ez [42:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:12).
> 
> **4\.** לִפְנֵי, prop. *at the face* or *front of,* the most general word for *in the presence of, before:* as [Gn 18:22](https://ref.ly/Gen18:22) עֹמֵד לִפְנֵי אַבְרָהָם, [Ex 4:21](https://ref.ly/Exod4:21), [11:10](https://ref.ly/Exod11:10), [2 S 2:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:14), [Pr 17:18](https://ref.ly/Prov17:18) \+ oft.; after a vb. of motion, [Gn 47:2](https://ref.ly/Gen47:2) וַיַּצִּיגֵם ´לפני פ and set them *before* Ph., [27:20](https://ref.ly/Gen27:20) ´כִּי הִקְרָה י לפני lit. made (it) to meet *before* me (cf. [24:12](https://ref.ly/Gen24:12)), [Ex 29:10](https://ref.ly/Exod29:10), [Pr 18:16](https://ref.ly/Prov18:16) etc. In partic., **a.** With the implication of (*a*) *under the eye* or *oversight of,* [Dt 25:2](https://ref.ly/Deut25:2), [1 S 3:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:1). (*b*) *under the eye and regard of,* [Gn 17:18](https://ref.ly/Gen17:18) לוּ יִשְׁמָעֵאל יִחְיֶה לְפָנֶיְ, [Ho 6:2](https://ref.ly/Hos6:2), [Is 53:2](https://ref.ly/Isa53:2), [Psalm 61:8](https://ref.ly/Ps61:8); cf. [Je 30:20](https://ref.ly/Jer30:20), [31:36](https://ref.ly/Jer31:36), [Is 66:22](https://ref.ly/Isa66:22), [Psalm 102:29](https://ref.ly/Ps102:29): also [1 S 2:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:28), [2 S 19:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:14). (*c*) fig. for *in* (or *into*) *the full* (mental) *view of,* [Gn 6:13](https://ref.ly/Gen6:13) קֵץ כָּל־בָּשָר בָּא לְפָנַי is come in *before me,* [La 1:22](https://ref.ly/Lam1:22) תָּבאֹ כָל־רָעָתָם לְפָנֶיְ, [Jon 1:2](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:2) עָֽלְתָה לְפָנַי (cf. אֶל [Gn 18:21](https://ref.ly/Gen18:21), [Ex 2:23](https://ref.ly/Exod2:23)), [Is 65:6](https://ref.ly/Isa65:6) כְּתוּבָה לְפָ\+ני, [Je 2:22](https://ref.ly/Jer2:22) נִכְתָּךְ לְפָנָי (cf. נֶגְדִּי [Is 47:12](https://ref.ly/Isa47:12)). (*d*) *openly before,* [1 S 12:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:2) הִתְהַלֵךְ לִפְנֵי, and with collat. idea of deserving (and receiving) regard [2:35](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:35), esp. ´לפני י [Gn 17:1](https://ref.ly/Gen17:1) al. (v. p. 236:a). (*e*) *in presence of* the moon or sun, [Psalm 72:5](https://ref.ly/Ps72:5), [72:17](https://ref.ly/Ps72:17), i.e. as long as they endure. (*f*) *free before, at the disposal of,* [Gn 13:9](https://ref.ly/Gen13:9) הֲלאֹ כָל־הָאָרֶץ לְפָנֶיְ (cf. [20:15](https://ref.ly/Gen20:15), [34:10](https://ref.ly/Gen34:10), [47:6](https://ref.ly/Gen47:6), [Je 40:4](https://ref.ly/Jer40:4), [2 Ch 14:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:6)), [24:51](https://ref.ly/Gen24:51), Ct [8:12](https://ref.ly/Song8:12). (*g*) *in the sight* (estimation) *of,* [Gn 7:1](https://ref.ly/Gen7:1) thee have I seen to be just *before* me, [10:9](https://ref.ly/Gen10:9) a mighty hunter *before* ´י, [Dt 24:4](https://ref.ly/Deut24:4) ´י ´תּוֺעֵבָה ל, v:[13](https://ref.ly/Deut24:13) ´י ´צְדָקָה ל, [1 S 20:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:1) b, אָרוּר ´י ´ל [Jos 6:26](https://ref.ly/Josh6:26), [1 S 26:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:19), ´י ´לְרָצוֺן ל acceptable *before* ´י, [Lv 1:3](https://ref.ly/Lev1:3) \+, [Psalm 19:15](https://ref.ly/Ps19:15); [2 K 5:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:1) a great man לִפְנֵי אֲדֹנָיו, [Pr 14:12](https://ref.ly/Prov14:12), [Psalm 143:2](https://ref.ly/Ps143:2); ´וַיִּיטַב ל (late syn. of earlier בְּעֵנֵי) † [Ne 2:5](https://ref.ly/Neh2:5), [2:6](https://ref.ly/Neh2:6), [Est 5:14](https://ref.ly/Esth5:14); ´טוֺב ל [Ec 2:26](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:26), [7:26](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:26); cf. also נָתַן לְרַחֲמִים לִפְנֵי, v. רַחֲמִים. (*h*) ´לִפְנֵי י, spec. of acts done with a solemn sense of ´י's presence, often, but not always, at a sanctuary: [Gn 27:7](https://ref.ly/Gen27:7) that I may eat and bless thee *before* ´י, before I die, [Ex 18:12](https://ref.ly/Exod18:12) to eat bread *before* God, [Dt 1:45](https://ref.ly/Deut1:45) ye wept *before* ´י, [6:25](https://ref.ly/Deut6:25), [12:7](https://ref.ly/Deut12:7) (to eat, so v:[18](https://ref.ly/Deut1:18), [14:23](https://ref.ly/Deut14:23), [14:26](https://ref.ly/Deut14:26), [15:20](https://ref.ly/Deut15:20)), v:[12](https://ref.ly/Deut15:12) (rejoice, so v:[18](https://ref.ly/Deut15:18), [27:7](https://ref.ly/Deut27:7), [Is 9:2](https://ref.ly/Isa9:2)), [Jos 18:6](https://ref.ly/Josh18:6), [24:1](https://ref.ly/Josh24:1), Ju [11:11](https://ref.ly/Judg11:11), [20:23](https://ref.ly/Judg20:23), [20:26](https://ref.ly/Judg20:26), [21:2](https://ref.ly/Judg21:2), [1 S 7:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:6), [10:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:19), [11:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:15), [12:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:7), [15:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:33), [23:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:18), [2 S 5:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:3), [7:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:18), [21:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:9); and constantly in P, as [Ex 16:9](https://ref.ly/Exod16:9), [16:33](https://ref.ly/Exod16:33), [16:34](https://ref.ly/Exod16:34), [Lv 1:5](https://ref.ly/Lev1:5), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Lev1:11), [3:1](https://ref.ly/Lev3:1), [3:7](https://ref.ly/Lev3:7) etc.; of residents in Jerus. [Is 23:18](https://ref.ly/Isa23:18). So in ´י ´הָלַךְ ל [1 K 2:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:4) al. (v. p. 234:b **c**). **b.** In other phrases:—(*a*) עָמַד לִפְנֵי *to stand before,* i.e. *wait upon, be in attendance on,* [Dt 1:38](https://ref.ly/Deut1:38) al. (v. עָמַד **1 e**). Simil. הָיָה לִפְנֵי [1 S 19:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:7) (cf. [29:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:8)), [2 K 5:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:2); ´עָבַד ל [2 S 16:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:19). (*b*) הִשְׁתַּחֲוָה לִפְנֵי *to bow down before* [Gn 23:12](https://ref.ly/Gen23:12) [Dt 26:10](https://ref.ly/Deut26:10), [2 K 18:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:22) al. (v. sub שָׁחָה; also נָפַל **3 b**): so with הִתְפַּלֵּל *to pray* [1 S 1:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:12) \+; cf. הלך פָני [1 K 12:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:30), [1 Ch 21:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:30). (*c*) *to be smitten* (נִגַּף) *before* a foe; v. נָגַף Niph. \[contrast נוּס מִפְּנֵי: infr. **6 a**]. So with נָפַל [1 S 14:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:13); נָחַן (in helplessness or flight), esp. in Dt, as [1:8](https://ref.ly/Deut1:8), [1:21](https://ref.ly/Deut1:21), [7:2](https://ref.ly/Deut7:2), [7:23](https://ref.ly/Deut7:23), [Jos 10:12](https://ref.ly/Josh10:12), [11:6](https://ref.ly/Josh11:6) \+ (DrDT lxxxii): cf. Ju [4:15](https://ref.ly/Judg4:15), [2 S 5:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:20), [Is 45:1](https://ref.ly/Isa45:1), [Je 1:17](https://ref.ly/Jer1:17). (*d*) עָמַד לִפְנֵי *to stand* (make a stand) *before* (not quite so strong as בִּפְנֵי ´ע, supr. **3**), [Ex 9:11](https://ref.ly/Exod9:11), Ju [2:14](https://ref.ly/Judg2:14), [2 K 10:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:4), [Je 49:19](https://ref.ly/Jer49:19), [Psalm 76:8](https://ref.ly/Ps76:8), [147:17](https://ref.ly/Ps147:17), [Na 1:6](https://ref.ly/Nah1:6); so with הִתְיַצֵּב [Dt 9:2](https://ref.ly/Deut9:2), [Jos 1:5](https://ref.ly/Josh1:5), [Jb 41:2](https://ref.ly/Job41:2), קוּם [Jos 7:12](https://ref.ly/Josh7:12), [7:13](https://ref.ly/Josh7:13). (*e*) in hostile sense (rare), יָצָא לִפְנֵי go forth *to face* [1 Ch 14:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:8), [2 Ch 14:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:9). (*f*) לִפְנֵי (נָתַן) שִים, of food, to set or place *before,* [Gn 18:8](https://ref.ly/Gen18:8), [24:33](https://ref.ly/Gen24:33) \+. (*g*) נָתַן לִפְנֵי to *set before,* i.e. propound for acceptance or choice, usu. of laws (esp. Dt Je), [Dt 4:8](https://ref.ly/Deut4:8), [11:26](https://ref.ly/Deut11:26), [11:32](https://ref.ly/Deut11:32), [30:1](https://ref.ly/Deut30:1), [30:15](https://ref.ly/Deut30:15), [30:19](https://ref.ly/Deut30:19), [1 K 9:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:6) (D2), [Je 9:12](https://ref.ly/Jer9:12), [21:8](https://ref.ly/Jer21:8), [26:4](https://ref.ly/Jer26:4), [44:10](https://ref.ly/Jer44:10), [Dn 9:10](https://ref.ly/Dan9:10), cf. Ez [23:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:24) (place at disposal of): so with שִים [Ex 19:7](https://ref.ly/Exod19:7), [21:1](https://ref.ly/Exod21:1). **c.** With reference to *position:* (*a*) *in front of, before,* esp. with vbs. of motion (with which נֶגֶד is hardly used), [Gn 32:21](https://ref.ly/Gen32:21) בַּמִּנְחָה הַהֹלֶכֶת לְפָנָ֔י that goeth *before* me, [33:14](https://ref.ly/Gen33:14) the cattle אֲשֶׁר לְפָנַי, [Ex 17:5](https://ref.ly/Exod17:5), [23:20](https://ref.ly/Exod23:20) etc.; in the description of a march or procession, [Ex 13:21](https://ref.ly/Exod13:21), [13:22](https://ref.ly/Exod13:22), [Nu 10:33](https://ref.ly/Num10:33), [Is 52:12](https://ref.ly/Isa52:12), [58:8](https://ref.ly/Isa58:8) \+; [2 S 3:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:31) and wail *before* Abner (before his bier); of flocks before a shepherd [Gn 32:18](https://ref.ly/Gen32:18); of captives or booty driven before a conqueror [1 S 30:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:20) (rd. וַיִּנְהֲגוּ לְפָנָיו We Dr), [Is 8:4](https://ref.ly/Isa8:4), [40:10](https://ref.ly/Isa40:10) \= [62:11](https://ref.ly/Isa62:11) וּפְעֻלָּתוֺ לְפָנָיו his recompence (i.e. his newly\-recovered people, regarded as his prize of war) *before* him, [Am 9:4](https://ref.ly/Amos9:4), [La 1:5](https://ref.ly/Lam1:5), [1:6](https://ref.ly/Lam1:6) (*b*) of a leader, etc. \= *at the head of,* [Dt 10:11](https://ref.ly/Deut10:11), Ju [9:39](https://ref.ly/Judg9:39), [1 S 8:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:20), [Mi 1:13](https://ref.ly/Mic1:13) al.; cf. בא ויצא לפני (העם) [Nu 27:17](https://ref.ly/Num27:17), [1 S 18:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:13), [18:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:16), [2 Ch 1:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:10); [Ec 4:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:16) *before* whom (at whose head) he was. (*c*) denoting superiority, *before, above,* [Gn 48:20](https://ref.ly/Gen48:20) וַיָּשֶם את־אפרים לפני מנשׁה, [Jb 34:19](https://ref.ly/Job34:19). **d.** Of *places* (not very common): [Ex 14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2) *before* Pi\-haḥiroth…, *before* Baal\-ẓephon, [Nu 33:7](https://ref.ly/Num33:7) b, [Gn 23:17](https://ref.ly/Gen23:17) מַמְרֵא ´אשׁר ל (usu. ´על פני מ: v. **7 a** *d*), [1 Ch 19:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:7), [Ne 8:1](https://ref.ly/Neh8:1), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Neh8:3); *before* the temple, veil, altar, etc., [Ex 16:34](https://ref.ly/Exod16:34) \+ הָעֵדֻת ´ל, [30:6](https://ref.ly/Exod30:6) \+ הַפָּרֹכֶת ´ל, [40:5](https://ref.ly/Exod40:5), [40:6](https://ref.ly/Exod40:6), [Nu 3:38](https://ref.ly/Num3:38), [7:10](https://ref.ly/Num7:10) al. (all P), [1 K 6:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:21), [7:49](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:49) \+. **e.** Of time, *before:* [Am 1:1](https://ref.ly/Amos1:1): לפני הָֽ רעַשׁ *before* the earthquake, [Gn 29:26](https://ref.ly/Gen29:26), [30:30](https://ref.ly/Gen30:30) לְפָנַי *before* me (i.e. before I came to thee), [Ex 10:14](https://ref.ly/Exod10:14) לפניו לא היה כן *before* it there was not the like, [Nu 13:22](https://ref.ly/Num13:22), [1 K 16:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:25), [16:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:30) מִכֹּל סֲשֶׁר לְפָנָיו, [Is 43:10](https://ref.ly/Isa43:10), [48:7](https://ref.ly/Isa48:7), [Pr 8:25](https://ref.ly/Prov8:25), [Jb 15:7](https://ref.ly/Job15:7) etc. Sq. inf., [Gn 13:10](https://ref.ly/Gen13:10) ´לִפְנֵי שַׁחֵת י *before* ´י's destroying, etc., [27:7](https://ref.ly/Gen27:7) לִפְנֵי מוֺתִי, [36:31](https://ref.ly/Gen36:31), [1 S 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:15) \+. Once, in late Heb., לִפְנֵי מִזֶּה *before* this [Ne 13:4](https://ref.ly/Neh13:4). **f.** *In the manner of, like* (rare and dub.), [1 S 1:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:16) אַל־תִּתֵּן אֶת־אֲמָֽתְ לִפְנֵי בַת־בִּלִיַּעַל *like* a worthless woman, [Jb 3:24](https://ref.ly/Job3:24) *like* my food, [4:19](https://ref.ly/Job4:19) they are crushed *like* the moth (cf. Lat *pro*). **5\.** מִלִּפְנֵי:73 *from before*:—**a.** *from the presence of* (properly, from a position *before* a person or object: to be distinguished from מִפְּנֵי; v. **6**): (*a*) [Gn 41:46](https://ref.ly/Gen41:46) ´ויצא יוסף מִלִּפְנֵי פ *from before* Ph., [47:10](https://ref.ly/Gen47:10), [2 K 5:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:27), [6:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:32) \+; ´י ´מל [Gn 4:6](https://ref.ly/Gen4:6) Cain went forth *from before* ´י, [Jon 1:3](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:3), [1:10](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:10); spec. of the sanctuary, [Lv 9:24](https://ref.ly/Lev9:24) ´ותצא אשׁ מלפני י (so [10:2](https://ref.ly/Lev10:2)), קֶצֶף [Nu 17:11](https://ref.ly/Num17:11) (cf. [2 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:2)), with לָקַח etc. [Lv 16:12](https://ref.ly/Lev16:12), [Nu 17:24](https://ref.ly/Num17:24), [20:9](https://ref.ly/Num20:9), [1 S 21:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:7) the shew\-bread that was removed *from before* ´י; *from before* a place [1 K 8:54](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:54), [Ezr 10:6](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:6), [2 Ch 1:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:13), (*b*) of taking, etc., *from before* a person, [Gn 23:4](https://ref.ly/Gen23:4) to bury my dead *from before* me, v:[8](https://ref.ly/Gen23:8), [Ex 36:3](https://ref.ly/Exod36:3), [Dt 17:18](https://ref.ly/Deut17:18) ´וכתב לו מִשְׁנֵה התורה הואת מל הכהנים *from* (the copy) *before* the priests, [28:31](https://ref.ly/Deut28:31) גָּזוּכ מלפניך. (*c*) with בקשׁ [Est 4:8](https://ref.ly/Esth4:8) \[cf. מִן קֳָדָם [Dn 2:18](https://ref.ly/Dan2:18)]; and to express the idea of *proceeding* from a person, [Est 1:19](https://ref.ly/Esth1:19) \[cf. [Dn 2:15](https://ref.ly/Dan2:15)], [Ec 10:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:5); [1 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12) הָעשֶׁר והכבוד מלפניך (cf. [Acts 3:19](https://ref.ly/Acts3:19)); [Psalm 17:2](https://ref.ly/Ps17:2) מלפניך. יֵצֵא משׁפטי. (*d*) of cutting off, etc., *from before* ´י (cf. לִפְנֵי **a** *b, c*), [Lv 22:3](https://ref.ly/Lev22:3), [1 K 8:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:25) לאֹ־יִכָּרֵת לְ אִישׁ, מִלְּפָנִי [Je 31:36](https://ref.ly/Jer31:36), [33:18](https://ref.ly/Jer33:18), [Is 48:19](https://ref.ly/Isa48:19): [Psalm 51:13](https://ref.ly/Ps51:13) אַל־תַּשְׁלִיכֵנִי מִלְּפָנְיְ (cf. **8 a**); [Je 16:17](https://ref.ly/Jer16:17) לאֹ נִסְתְּרוּ מִלְּפָנַי, [18:23](https://ref.ly/Jer18:23), [Ne 3:37](https://ref.ly/Neh3:37). (*e*) of expelling *from before* an invader, [Ex 23:28](https://ref.ly/Exod23:28), [Dt 9:4](https://ref.ly/Deut9:4), [11:23](https://ref.ly/Deut11:23), [31:3](https://ref.ly/Deut31:3) \+ (but much less common than מִפְּנֵי). † **b.** (Chiefly late) to express the *source* or *cause,* with the force of the לְ weakened, in cases where the earlier language would have used מִפְּנֵי: with vbs. of fearing, [1 S 18:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:12), [Ec 3:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:14), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:12), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:13);
