v. also Köii. 320\. **6\.** מִפְּנֵי *from the face* or *presence of, from before,* [Ex 14:19](https://ref.ly/Exod14:19) וַיִּסַּע עמוד הענן מִפְּנֵיהֶם removecl *from before* them, [Is 30:11](https://ref.ly/Isa30:11), [Ho 11:2](https://ref.ly/Hos11:2); so **a.** (oft. with suggestion of causation (v. מִן **2 d, e, f**), *because of, for fear of* \[so AV RV Ju [9:21](https://ref.ly/Judg9:21), [1 S 21:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:11), [23:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:26), [Je 35:11](https://ref.ly/Jer35:11), [35:11](https://ref.ly/Jer35:11), [37:11](https://ref.ly/Jer37:11), [41:9](https://ref.ly/Jer41:9), [50:16](https://ref.ly/Jer50:16)]) after vbs. of hiding [Gn 3:8](https://ref.ly/Gen3:8), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14), [Is 2:10](https://ref.ly/Isa2:10) (cf. v:[19](https://ref.ly/Isa2:19), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Isa2:21)); and esp. of escaping, Ju [9:21](https://ref.ly/Judg9:21) (Thes ‘ bene Vulgate *ob metum* A.’), [1 S 18:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:11), [19:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:10), [23:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:26), or fleeing, [Ex 4:3](https://ref.ly/Exod4:3), [14:25](https://ref.ly/Exod14:25), [1 S 17:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:24) \+ oft. (v. בָּרַךְ and נוּס), and expelling, destroying, etc. [Ex 23:30](https://ref.ly/Exod23:30) אֲגָֽרְשֶׁנּוּ מִפָּנֶיְ I will expel them *from before* thee, [34:11](https://ref.ly/Exod34:11), [34:24](https://ref.ly/Exod34:24) אוֺרִישׁ גּוֺיִם מִפָּנֶיְ (v. הוֺרִישׁ), [Lv 18:24](https://ref.ly/Lev18:24), [Dt 8:20](https://ref.ly/Deut8:20), [Am 2:9](https://ref.ly/Amos2:9), [Psalm 89:24](https://ref.ly/Ps89:24) \+ oft. (as though clearing the way for Israel, for Israel's sake: cf. [Is 63:12](https://ref.ly/Isa63:12) בּוֺקֵעַ מַיִס מִפְּנֵיהֶם, [Jos 2:10](https://ref.ly/Josh2:10), [4:23](https://ref.ly/Josh4:23)). Pregn. [Is 17:9](https://ref.ly/Isa17:9) ´אשׁר עזבו מפני בני ישר …כַּעֲזוּבַת which they forsook (fleeing) *from before,* etc. **b.** implying causation more distinctly. [Psalm 9:4](https://ref.ly/Ps9:4) יאבדו מפניך perish *from before* thee or *at the presence,* [Dt 7:20](https://ref.ly/Deut7:20): very oft. with vbs. of fearing, as גור, יגר, חיל, ירא, ערץ, פחד, קוץ [Ex 9:30](https://ref.ly/Exod9:30), [Nu 22:3](https://ref.ly/Num22:3), [22:3](https://ref.ly/Num22:3), [Dt 1:17](https://ref.ly/Deut1:17), [Je 5:22](https://ref.ly/Jer5:22) etc. (mostly when the object inspiring fear is a person: when it is a thing, מִן alone is preferred; but see [Dt 5:5](https://ref.ly/Deut5:5), [9:19](https://ref.ly/Deut9:19)); of being alarmed or dismayed [Gn 45:3](https://ref.ly/Gen45:3), [Jos 2:9](https://ref.ly/Josh2:9), [Je 1:17](https://ref.ly/Jer1:17), [Jb 23:15](https://ref.ly/Job23:15), etc.; of humbling oneself [Ex 10:3](https://ref.ly/Exod10:3), Ju [11:33](https://ref.ly/Judg11:33) \+ ; with other words, to indicate the *external ground* of an action or effect, as הַס מִפָּנָיו be silent *at* (i.e. *because of*) his presence [Zp 1:7](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:7), Hb [2:20](https://ref.ly/Hab2:20); Ju [5:5](https://ref.ly/Judg5:5) the mts. flowed ´מפני י *at the presence of* ´י, [Is 63:19](https://ref.ly/Isa63:19), [Je 4:26](https://ref.ly/Jer4:26), [Na 1:5](https://ref.ly/Nah1:5), [Psalm 68:3](https://ref.ly/Ps68:3) as wax melteth מפני אשׁ; [Dt 20:19](https://ref.ly/Deut20:19); [Is 19:1](https://ref.ly/Isa19:1); [26:17](https://ref.ly/Isa26:17) כן היינו מפניך so were we *through thy presence* (more than לפניך,—implying that their state was *due to* ´י); קום מפני to rise up *at the presence of* [Gn 31:35](https://ref.ly/Gen31:35), [Lv 19:32](https://ref.ly/Lev19:32). (N.B. In **a, b,** freq. rendered *before* in AV RV, and so confused with לפני; but the מִן retains always its full force in the Heb.) **c.** of the *cause,* whether nearer or more remote: (*a*) [Gn 6:13](https://ref.ly/Gen6:13) מָֽלְאָה הָאָרֶץ חָמָס מִפְּנֵיהֶם *because of* them (i.e. occasioned by them), [27:4](https://ref.ly/Gen27:4) I loathe my life *because of* the daughters of Heth, [36:7](https://ref.ly/Gen36:7) the land could not support them *because of* their cattle, [41:31](https://ref.ly/Gen41:31), [47:13](https://ref.ly/Gen47:13), [Ex 3:7](https://ref.ly/Exod3:7) their cry *by reason of* their taskmasters, [8:20](https://ref.ly/Exod8:20) תִּשָּׁחֵת הָאָרֶץ מִפְּנֵי הֶעָרֹב, [9:11](https://ref.ly/Exod9:11), [Jos 2:11](https://ref.ly/Josh2:11), [23:3](https://ref.ly/Josh23:3), Ju [2:18](https://ref.ly/Judg2:18), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Judg6:6), [1 K 5:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:17), [8:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:11) \+ oft.; [Is 10:27](https://ref.ly/Isa10:27) (RVm), [Je 15:17](https://ref.ly/Jer15:17), [Psalm 38:4](https://ref.ly/Ps38:4), [38:6](https://ref.ly/Ps38:6), etc.; [Je 4:4](https://ref.ly/Jer4:4) \+ מִפְּנֵי רֹעַ מַעַלְלֵיכֶם *by reason of* the evil of your doings, [7:12](https://ref.ly/Jer7:12), [9:6](https://ref.ly/Jer9:6), [13:17](https://ref.ly/Jer13:17), [14:16](https://ref.ly/Jer14:16), [23:9](https://ref.ly/Jer23:9) \+ oft. (*b*) stating the reason for which a thing is *not* done (Lat. *prae,*), [1 K 8:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:11), [Jb 37:19](https://ref.ly/Job37:19). †(*c*) with אֲשֶׁר as a **conj.,** twice, *because that,* [Ex 19:18](https://ref.ly/Exod19:18), [Je 44:23](https://ref.ly/Jer44:23).— [Nu 33:8](https://ref.ly/Num33:8) v. פִּי הַחִירֹת. **7\.** עַל־פְּנֵי (cf. Ph. עַל פְּנֵ, עלת פְּנֵ, Lzb352) has different meanings according to the different senses of the noun and the prep.:—**a.** From the sense of *face* or *front:* (*a*) *in front of* (more definite and distinct than לפני): [Psalm 18:43](https://ref.ly/Ps18:43) as dust *before* the wind (cf. לפני [35:5](https://ref.ly/Ps35:5), [83:14](https://ref.ly/Ps83:14) \+), [Gn 32:22](https://ref.ly/Gen32:22) the present passed on על־פניו *in front of* him, [Ex 33:19](https://ref.ly/Exod33:19) אעביר כל־טוּבי על־פניך,[2 S 15:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:18), [15:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:23) (read על־פניו: v. Dr), [Jb 4:15](https://ref.ly/Job4:15); \= *in the sight of,* [Lv 10:3](https://ref.ly/Lev10:3) ועל־פני כל העם אֶכָּבֵד, [Je 6:7](https://ref.ly/Jer6:7) עַל־פָּנַי תָּמִיד חֳלִי וּמַכָּה, [13:26](https://ref.ly/Jer13:26) (cf. [Na 3:5](https://ref.ly/Nah3:5)), [Psalm 9:20](https://ref.ly/Ps9:20) יִשָּֽׁפְטוּ נוים על־פניך *before* thee: defiantly, [Jb 1:11](https://ref.ly/Job1:11) אם־לא ע־ל פניך יְבָֽרְכֶָ֑ will renounce thee *to thy face,* [6:28](https://ref.ly/Job6:28) ועל־פניכם אם אֲכַוֵּב, [21:31](https://ref.ly/Job21:31); to die *in the presence of* any one ( \= in his lifetime) † [Gn 11:28](https://ref.ly/Gen11:28), [Nu 3:4](https://ref.ly/Num3:4). (*b*) *before,* to the exclusion of another, i.e. *in preference to,* [Dt 21:16](https://ref.ly/Deut21:16), and perh.(Ew§ 217 *l*) [Ex 20:3](https://ref.ly/Exod20:3) ([Dt 5:7](https://ref.ly/Deut5:7)) לא יהיה לך אלהים אחרים על־פני (Thes al. as *c*). (*c*) *in addition to,* [Jb 16:14](https://ref.ly/Job16:14) פֶּרֶץ עַל־פְּנֵי־פֶרֶץ יִפְרְצֵנִי. (*d*) of localities, *in front of,* mostly (but not always: v. GFMJu., p. 351\) \= *east of,* [1 K 6:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:3) the porch *in front of,* etc., v:[3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:3), [7:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:6), [8:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:8), [2 Ch 3:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:17), Ez [42:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:8); [Gn 16:12](https://ref.ly/Gen16:12) על־פני כל־אחיו ישׁכן (cf. [25:18](https://ref.ly/Gen25:18) b), perh. (Di al.) with collateral idea of defiance; מַמְרֵא ´עַל־פ [23:19](https://ref.ly/Gen23:19) ([50:13](https://ref.ly/Gen50:13)), [Ex 16:14](https://ref.ly/Exod16:14); oft. in …אשׁר על־פני [Gn 25:9](https://ref.ly/Gen25:9) ([49:30](https://ref.ly/Gen49:30)), מצרים v:[18](https://ref.ly/Gen25:18) a ([Jos 13:3](https://ref.ly/Josh13:3), [1 S 15:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:7)), [Nu 21:11](https://ref.ly/Num21:11), [33:7](https://ref.ly/Num33:7) a, ירחו [Dt 32:49](https://ref.ly/Deut32:49) ([34:1](https://ref.ly/Deut34:1)), [Jos 13:3](https://ref.ly/Josh13:3), [13:25](https://ref.ly/Josh13:25), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8) ([18:16](https://ref.ly/Josh18:16)), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Josh17:7), [18:14](https://ref.ly/Josh18:14), [19:11](https://ref.ly/Josh19:11), Ju [16:3](https://ref.ly/Judg16:3), [1 S 24:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:3) (prob.), [26:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:1), [26:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:3), [2 S 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:24), [1 K 11:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:7) (the Mt. of Olives, E. of Jews.; so [2 K 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:13), [Zc 14:4](https://ref.ly/Zech14:4)), [17:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:3), [17:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:5).—[Gn 1:20](https://ref.ly/Gen1:20) let fowl fly על־פני רקיע השׁמים *in front of* the firmament of heaven, viz. as looked up to from below, i.e. between the firmament and the earth. (*e*) with vbs. implying direction, *over towards,* [Gn 18:16](https://ref.ly/Gen18:16) וישׁקף על־פני סדם, [19:28](https://ref.ly/Gen19:28); [Nu 21:20](https://ref.ly/Num21:20) ([23:28](https://ref.ly/Num23:28)) נשׁקפה על־פני הישׁימן *overlooking;* to sprinkle *against the front of* [Lv 16:14](https://ref.ly/Lev16:14); in a hostile sense (rare), [Na 2:2](https://ref.ly/Nah2:2) עָלָה מֵפִיץ עַל־פָּנַיִךְ, [Psalm 21:13](https://ref.ly/Ps21:13), Ez [32:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:10). **b.** From the sense of *surface,* [Gn 1:2](https://ref.ly/Gen1:2) על־פני תהום *upon the face* of the deep, [Ex 16:14](https://ref.ly/Exod16:14) ´המדבר על־פ: oft. in the phrases הארץ (כל)על־פני [Gn 1:29](https://ref.ly/Gen1:29), [7:3](https://ref.ly/Gen7:3) \+, and (more freq.) על־פני האדמה *on the face* of the ground [6:1](https://ref.ly/Gen6:1), [7:23](https://ref.ly/Gen7:23) etc.; על פני השדה [Lv 17:5](https://ref.ly/Lev17:5), [Nu 19:16](https://ref.ly/Num19:16), [1 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:25) \+ ; after vbs. of motion, [11:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:4) lest we be scattered *over the face* of all the earth, v:[8](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:8), v:[9](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:9), [Ex 32:20](https://ref.ly/Exod32:20), [Lv 14:7](https://ref.ly/Lev14:7) (v:[53](https://ref.ly/Lev14:53) אל), [Is 18:2](https://ref.ly/Isa18:2), [19:8](https://ref.ly/Isa19:8), [Am 5:8](https://ref.ly/Amos5:8), Ez [32:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:4), [Jb 5:10](https://ref.ly/Job5:10) \+. **8\.** מֵעַל־פְּנֵי **a.** *from before the face of,* [Gn 23:3](https://ref.ly/Gen23:3) Abraham rose up מֵעַל פְּנֵי מֵתוֺ *from before* his dead, i.e. from mourning before or beside it; Psalm (פניו) מֵעַל פני  *from before my (his) face,* oft. in K Je of the rejection of Isr. or the temple, as with שִׁלַּת [1 K 9:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:7) (‖ [2 Ch 7:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:20) השׁליך), [Je 15:1](https://ref.ly/Jer15:1), השׁליד [2 K 13:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs13:23), [24:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:20) ([Je 52:3](https://ref.ly/Jer52:3)), [Je 7:15](https://ref.ly/Jer7:15), נטשׁ [Je 23:39](https://ref.ly/Jer23:39), הסיל [2 K 17:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:18), [17:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:23), [23:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:27), [24:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:3), [Je 32:31](https://ref.ly/Jer32:31). **b.** *from of the surface of,* oft. in the phrase מֵעַל פְּנֵי הָאֲדָמָה, with verbs of cutting off, removing, expelling, etc., Psalm [Gn 4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Gen6:7), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Gen7:4), [8:8](https://ref.ly/Gen8:8), [Ex 32:12](https://ref.ly/Exod32:12), [Dt 6:15](https://ref.ly/Deut6:15), [1 S 20:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:15), [1 K 9:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:7), [13:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:34), [Am 9:8](https://ref.ly/Amos9:8), [Je 28:16](https://ref.ly/Jer28:16), [Zp 1:2](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:2), [1:3](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:3).

> \[Str6441] † פְּנִ֫ימָה **adv.** (ה loc.) **1\.** towards the (in\-) side. **2\.** within (lit. *faceward,* fr. point of view of one entering by opposite door);—of a building, usu. temple: **1\.** after vb. of motion [Lv 10:18](https://ref.ly/Lev10:18), [2 Ch 29:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:18), and (after הִגִּיד) [2 K 7:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:11), also ´לִפ Ez [41:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:3), [2 Ch 29:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:16). **2\.** ´פ [1 K 6:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:18), also ´לִפ v:[30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:30), so read also v:[29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:29) (for MT מִלִּפְנִים, v. KmpKau Kit Benz), Ez [40:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:16), [40:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:16), also [Psalm 45:14](https://ref.ly/Ps45:14) MT, i.e. within the house, but Krochm Gr Che al. (plausibly) פְּנִינִים, q.v.; ´מִפּ [1 K 6:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:19), [6:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:21).\= [2 Ch 3:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:4).

> \[Str6442] פְּנִימִי:32 **adj.** inner ;—´פ [1 K 6:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:27) \+; f. פְּנִימִית v:[36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:36) \+ ; mpl. פְּנִימִים [1 Ch 28:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:11); fpl. פְּנִימִיּוֺת [2 Ch 4:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:22); —a1w. c. art., and alw. of parts of building, usu. temple : [1 K 6:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:27), [7:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:12) \+ ; esp. Ez [8:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:3), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:16), [10:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:3) \+ 21 t. 40–46 ; as subst. בַּפְּנִימִי [41:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:17) *on the inside* (but dub., v. Co al.); אֶל־הַפְּנִימִית [42:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:4) *toward the inside* (del. LXX. SyrVer Co Toy al.).

> \[Str3942,6440] לִפְָ\+ני [1 K 6:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:17), Ew§ 164 a Ke as adj., *anterior,* but rd., with LXX, הַדְּבִיד Th Sta Kit al.

> \[Str6439] † פְּנוּאֵל **n.pr.** (*face of God,* cf. GFM: Ju [8:8](https://ref.ly/Judg8:8); late As. *Panîli* HilprUniv. Pa. Exp. ix. 68\);—Φανουηλ, but in Gn εἶδος (τοῦ) θεοῦ: **1\. loc.** E. of Jordan, near Jabbok [Gn 32:32](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32), Ju [8:8](https://ref.ly/Judg8:8), [8:8](https://ref.ly/Judg8:8), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Judg8:9), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Judg8:17), [1 K 12:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:25) \= פְּנִיאֵל [Gn 32:31](https://ref.ly/Gen32:31): site unknown; v. esp. PaineBib.Sac.1878,481 ff. **2\. m.a.** in Judah [1 Ch 4:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:4). **b.** in Benjamin [1 Ch 8:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:25) Qr (Kt פניאל).

> \[Str3312] יְפֻנֶּה:16 **n.pr.m.** (on form cf. LagBN 134\);—**1\.** father of Caleb [Nu 13:6](https://ref.ly/Num13:6), [14:6](https://ref.ly/Num14:6), [Jos 14:6](https://ref.ly/Josh14:6), [14:13](https://ref.ly/Josh14:13), [1 Ch 4:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:15) \+ 10 t., Ιεφοννη. **2\.** Asherite [1 Ch 7:38](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:38), Ιφινα, A Ιεφιηλ, LXXL Ιεφοννη.

## פנן (id: BDB07747)

פנן (√ of foll.; appar. ‖ from of פנה).

> \[Str6434,6438] פִּנָּה:28 **n.f.** corner;—abs. ´פ [2 K 14:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:13) \+, cstr. פִּנַּת [Je 31:40](https://ref.ly/Jer31:40) \+ ; sf. פִּנָּתָהּ [Jb 38:9](https://ref.ly/Job38:9), פִּנָּהּ [Pr 7:8](https://ref.ly/Prov7:8) (Ges§ 91 e); pl. פִּנּוֺת abs. [Zp 1:16](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:16), [2 Ch 26:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:15), פִּנִּים [Zc 14:10](https://ref.ly/Zech14:10); cstr. פִּנּוֺת [1 K 7:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:34) \+, etc.;—**1\. a.** *corner,* of square objects [1 K 7:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:34) \+ v:[30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:30) (rd. פִּנּוֺתָיו for פַּעֲמֹתָיו, cf. Kit), [Ex 27:2](https://ref.ly/Exod27:2), [38:2](https://ref.ly/Exod38:2), Ez [43:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:19); of house [Jb 1:19](https://ref.ly/Job1:19), roof [Pr 7:12](https://ref.ly/Prov7:12), [21:9](https://ref.ly/Prov21:9) \= [25:24](https://ref.ly/Prov25:24); ´אֶבֶן לְפ [Je 51:26](https://ref.ly/Jer51:26) i.e. a corner\-stone; ´אֶבֶן פ of earth [Jb 38:9](https://ref.ly/Job38:9); cf. (fig.) [Psalm 118:22](https://ref.ly/Ps118:22), and (אבן om.) [Is 28:16](https://ref.ly/Isa28:16); ´פ \= *street\-corner* [2 Ch 28:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:24). **b.** specif. of wall of Jerus., [Ne 3:24](https://ref.ly/Neh3:24) (in SE.), v:[31](https://ref.ly/Neh3:31), v:[32](https://ref.ly/Neh3:32) (NE.); ´שַׁעַד הַפּ 2 K 14:l3 \+ ‖ [2 Ch 25:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:23) LXX SyrVer Targum and most (for MT הַפּוֺנֶה ´שׁ), \+ (v. שַׁעַר); appar. with battlements [2 Ch 26:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:15); of cities in gen. הַנְּבֹהוֺת ´הַפּ [Zp 1:16](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:16); of the nations [3:6](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:6). **2\.** fig. of chief, ruler as *corner* (support or defence), Ju [20:2](https://ref.ly/Judg20:2), [1 S 14:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:38), [Is 19:13](https://ref.ly/Isa19:13) (rd. perhaps pl.); [Zc 10:4](https://ref.ly/Zech10:4) (\+ יָתֵד q.v.).

> \[Str6443] † פְּנִינִים **n.\[f.]pl.** corals (? as *branching* cf. Ar. فَنَنٌ *branch* of tree; so Mich Thes al.; \> *pearls* BoHieroz. 601 DIJb after Ra al.);—´פ fig. of value [Pr 20:15](https://ref.ly/Prov20:15); usu. ´מִפּ, camp. [Jb 28:18](https://ref.ly/Job28:18), [Pr 3:15](https://ref.ly/Prov3:15) (Kt מפניים), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Prov8:11), [31:10](https://ref.ly/Prov31:10) (all of value), [La 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lam4:7) (of red colour); \+ [Psalm 45:14](https://ref.ly/Ps45:14) (perh.; for פְּנִימָה, q. v.).

> \[Str6444] † פְּנִנָּה **n.pr.f.** (prop. sg. of foregoing ?) wife of Elkanah [1 S 1:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:2), [1:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:2), [1:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:4), Φεννανα.

## I פָּסַח (id: BDB07756)

\[Str6452] † I. פָּסַח **vb.** pass or spring over ;—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´פ [Ex 12:27](https://ref.ly/Exod12:27), ´וּפ consec. v:[23](https://ref.ly/Exod12:23) (J); 1 s. וּפָסַחְתִּ֫י v:[13](https://ref.ly/Exod12:13) (P), all c. עַל, of ´י *passing over* (sparing) houses of Isr.; *Inf. abs.* פָּסוֺחַ [Is 31:5](https://ref.ly/Isa31:5) *a passing over* (SC. עַל־יְרוּשָׁלַםִ).

> \[Str6453] † פֶּ֫סַח **n.m.** passover (usu. (Thes and most) from I. פסח, with reference to [Ex 12:13](https://ref.ly/Exod12:13) etc.; ReussGesch. AT § 58 *passing over* into new year; SchaeferPassah\-Mazzoth (1900\), 346 cp. As. *pašâḫu, soothe, placate* \[deity], *\< be soothed,* Pi. trans., JenZA iv (1889\), 275 ZimBab. Rel. i (1896\). Gloss.; ii (1899\), 92; SchwallyIdiot, 124 fr. II. פסח, of sacred dance);—abs. ´פ [Ex 12:11](https://ref.ly/Exod12:11) \+ ; פָּסַח v:[21](https://ref.ly/Exod12:21) \+ ; pl. פְּסָחִים [2 Ch 30:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:17) \+ 3 t.;—**1\.** *sacrifice of Passover,* involving communion\-meal, hence a species of peace\-offering (cf. I. זֶבַח **II. 2**), ´הוא לי ´זֶבַח פ Ex [12:27](https://ref.ly/Exod12:27) (J), cf. v:[11](https://ref.ly/Exod12:11) (P); ´וָבַח פ [Dt 16:2](https://ref.ly/Deut16:2), [16:5](https://ref.ly/Deut16:5), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Deut16:6); ´אבל הפ [2 Ch 30:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:18); the special feature lay in the application of blood to homes to consecrate them: cf. sim. Bab. rite of purification Zimib. ii. 126–7\. **2\.** *the animal victim of the passover :* ´שׁחט פ [Ex 12:21](https://ref.ly/Exod12:21) (J) [2 Ch 30:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:15), [35:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:1), [35:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:6), [35:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:11), [Ez 6:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:20), cf. [2 Ch 30:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:17); באשׁ ´בשׁל הפ [2 Ch 35:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:13); לפסחים [2 Ch 35:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:7), [35:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:8), [35:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:9). \[Passover animals (cf. BrHex.206\) were צאֹן *flock* [Ex 12:21](https://ref.ly/Exod12:21) (J), שֶׁה v:[3](https://ref.ly/Exod12:3), v:[4](https://ref.ly/Exod12:4), v:[5](https://ref.ly/Exod12:5), incl. כֶּבֶשׁ and עֵז v:[5](https://ref.ly/Exod12:5) (P); צאן ובקר [Dt 16:2](https://ref.ly/Deut16:2); large numbers of all these (שֶה not used) in Josiah's Passover, act. to [2 Ch 35:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:7), [35:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:8), [35:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:9), but evident mingling of whole burnt\-offerings for the passover with special passover victim.] **3\.** *festival of the passover :* ´חג הפ [Ex 34:25](https://ref.ly/Exod34:25) (J); ´פ(ה) [Lv 23:5](https://ref.ly/Lev23:5), [Nu 28:16](https://ref.ly/Num28:16), [33:3](https://ref.ly/Num33:3), [Jos 5:11](https://ref.ly/Josh5:11) (P) Ez [45:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:21); ´חֻקַּת הפ [Ex 12:43](https://ref.ly/Exod12:43), [Nu 9:12](https://ref.ly/Num9:12), [9:14](https://ref.ly/Num9:14) (P); ´עשה פ [Ex 12:48](https://ref.ly/Exod12:48), [Nu 9:2](https://ref.ly/Num9:2), [9:4](https://ref.ly/Num9:4), [9:5](https://ref.ly/Num9:5), [9:6](https://ref.ly/Num9:6), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Num9:10), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Num9:13), [9:14](https://ref.ly/Num9:14), [Jos 5:10](https://ref.ly/Josh5:10) (P) [Dt 16:1](https://ref.ly/Deut16:1), [2 K 23:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:21), [23:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:22), [23:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:23), [2 Ch 30:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:1), [30:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:2), [30:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:5), [35:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:1), [35:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:16), [35:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:17), [35:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:18), [35:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:18), [35:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:19), [Ezr 6:19](https://ref.ly/Ezra6:19); it was held in month הָאָבִיב [Dt 16:1](https://ref.ly/Deut16:1), בָּעָרֶב v:[6](https://ref.ly/Deut16:6); on 14th day [Jos 5:10](https://ref.ly/Josh5:10) (P), of 1st month Ez [45:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:21); בֵּין הערבים([Ex 12:6](https://ref.ly/Exod12:6)) [Lv 23:5](https://ref.ly/Lev23:5), [Nu 9:5](https://ref.ly/Num9:5) (P); if impossible at that time, then on 14th of 2nd month [Nu 9:10](https://ref.ly/Num9:10), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Num9:12). \[No ref. to פֶּסַח in E ; J subordinates it to מַצּוֺת (the great feast of JE); in D it predom. over מצות; 1' makes it first in importance (Br: Hex. 195 f.)].]

## II פָּסַח (id: BDB07759)

\[Str6452] † II. \[פָּסַח] **vb.** limp (Thes sub I. ´פ but dub.; Ar. فَسَخَ is *dislocate;* acc. to Gerber73 II. ´פ is denom. fr. פִּסֵּחַ \[and this sub I. ´פ?]);—**Qal** *Pt.* pl. פֹּסְחִים עַל־שְׁתֵּי הַסְּעַפִּים [1 K 18:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:21) fig. (v. \[סְעִפָּה]). **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיִּפֹּל וַיִּפָּסֵחַ [2 S 4:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:4) *and he fell and was made limping* (lame). **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 mpl. וַיְפַמְּחוּ עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּחַ [1 K 18:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:26) *they went limping* (along by the altar), i.e. prob. in their dance, v. Kit PietschmPhön. 220 (Gr ויפסעו \[ \= וַיִּפְשְעוּ] *stepped*).

> \[Str6454] † פַּסֵחַ **n.pr.m.** (*limper*);—**1\.** in Judah [1 Ch 4:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:12), Βεσσηε, Φεσση, etc. **2\.** head of post\-ex. family [Ezr 2:49](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:49) \= [Ne 7:51](https://ref.ly/Neh7:51), Φισον, etc., LXXL Φασσα. **3\.** father of wall\-builder [Ne 3:6](https://ref.ly/Neh3:6), Φασεκ, Φεσσε.

> \[Str6455] † פִּסֵּחַ **adj.** lame;—abs. ´פ [2 S 9:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:13) \+ ; pl. פִּסְחִים [5:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:6) \+ ;—as attrib. [Lv 21:18](https://ref.ly/Lev21:18) (\+ עִוֵּר); pred. [2 S 19:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:27), שְׁתֵּי רַגְלָיו ´פ 9:13 *lame as to both his feet;* cf. also (of animal) [Dt 15:21](https://ref.ly/Deut15:21) (\+ עִוֵּר); as subst. [2 S 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:6), [5:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:8), [5:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:8), [Je 31:8](https://ref.ly/Jer31:8), [Mal 1:8](https://ref.ly/Mal1:8), [1:13](https://ref.ly/Mal1:13), [Jb 29:15](https://ref.ly/Job29:15) (all \+ עִוֵּר), also [Is 33:23](https://ref.ly/Isa33:23), [35:6](https://ref.ly/Isa35:6), [Pr 26:7](https://ref.ly/Prov26:7).

> \[Str6457] † פָּסָךְ (van d. H. פָּסַח) **n.pr.m.** Asherite [1 Ch 7:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:33), Βαισηχι, A Φεσηχι, LXXL Φασεχ.

## I פסס (id: BDB07766)

I. פסס (√ of foll.; perh. cp. פשת  *spread;* cf. Ph.(Pu.) פס *tablet;* Aram. פַסָּא, ܦܰܣܬܳܐ, \=BH).

> \[Str6446] † \[פַּס] **n.\[m.]** flat of hand or foot (palm, sole);—only כְּתֹנֶת פַּסִּים tunic reaching to palms and soles (v. ´כ) [Gn 37:3](https://ref.ly/Gen37:3), [37:23](https://ref.ly/Gen37:23), [37:32](https://ref.ly/Gen37:32), [2 S 13:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:18), [13:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:19) (cf. Dr).

> \[Str6451] † \[פִּסָּה] **n.f.** dub.; if correct, prob. abundance, plenty (? prop. *spread out*);—cstr. פִּסַּת בָּר [Psalm 72:16](https://ref.ly/Ps72:16); Lag Gr Che al. read שִׁפְעַת.

## פָּעָה (id: BDB07771)

\[Str6463] † \[פָּעָה] **vb.** groan (onomatop. acc. to Thes; NH Hiph. *bleat,* so Ar. بعى, بغى Dozy; Aram. פְּעָא ܦܥܳܐ *bleat,*);—*Impf.* 1 s. כַּיּוֺלֵדָה אֶפְעֶה [Is 42:14](https://ref.ly/Isa42:14) (of ´י staining himself to deliver Isr., \+ אֶשֹּׁם \[נשׁם], אֶשְׁאַף).—\[אֶפַע] [Is 41:26](https://ref.ly/Isa41:26). v.p. 67\.

> \[Str660] † אֶפְעֶה **n.\[m.]** a kind of viper (Thes ‘ a flando et sibilando;’ cf. Ar. أَفْغَي *viper,* Eth. አፍዖት፡ *id.*);—´א [Is 30:6](https://ref.ly/Isa30:6), [59:5](https://ref.ly/Isa59:5), ´לְשׁוֺן א [Jb 20:16](https://ref.ly/Job20:16).

> \[Str6464] † פָּעוּ, פָּעִי **n.pr.loc.** in Edom, פָּעוּ [Gn 36:39](https://ref.ly/Gen36:39) \= פָּעִי [1 Ch 1:50](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:50); Φογωρ, LXXL Ch Φαουα.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 59:5; Job 20:16; 1 Chronicles 1:50; Isaiah 42:14; Isaiah 30:6; Genesis 36:39; Isaiah 41:26

[Dt 33:11](https://ref.ly/Deut33:11) \+ ; sf. פָּעֳלוֺ 32:4 \+, פֹּעֲלוֺ [Is 1:31](https://ref.ly/Isa1:31) (Köii.1, 35, 493 Ges §, 93 q, v. infr.), [Je 22:13](https://ref.ly/Jer22:13); פָּעָלְכֶם [Is 41:24](https://ref.ly/Isa41:24), etc.; pl. פְּעָלִים [2 S 23:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:20), [1 Ch 11:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:22); —**1\.** *deed, thing done:* **a.** of God, פָּעֳלוֺ his work in providence [Dt 32:4](https://ref.ly/Deut32:4), [Is 5:12](https://ref.ly/Isa5:12), [Jb 36:24](https://ref.ly/Job36:24); in deliverance [Psalm 44:2](https://ref.ly/Ps44:2) (as acc. cogn.), [77:13](https://ref.ly/Ps77:13) \+ 5 t.; in judgment [64:10](https://ref.ly/Ps64:10), [95:9](https://ref.ly/Ps95:9), as acc. cogn. ## פָּעַם (id: BDB07780)

\[Str6470] \[פָּעַם] **vb.** thrust, impel (prob. orig. *strike, hit,* v. deriv., and Ph. פעם *foot*);—**Qal** *Inf. cstr.* sf. לְפַעֲמוֺ Ju [13:25](https://ref.ly/Judg13:25) the spirit of ´י began to *impel him.* **Niph.** *be distrurbed, Pf.* 1 s. נִפְעַמְתִּי [Psalm 77:5](https://ref.ly/Ps77:5); *Impf.* 3 fs. וַתִּפָּ֫עֶם רוּחוֺ [Gn 41:8](https://ref.ly/Gen41:8), [Dn 2:3](https://ref.ly/Dan2:3); so **Hithp.** *Impf.* 3 fs. וַתִּתְפָּ֫עֶם רוּחוֺ [Dn 2:1](https://ref.ly/Dan2:1).

> \[Str6471] פַּ֫עַם:118 **n.f.**:[Ex 8:28](https://ref.ly/Exod8:28) (masc. only Ju [16:23](https://ref.ly/Judg16:23) \[text suspicious, v. GFM AlbrZAW xvi (1896\), 76 Kösynt. § 248m], [2 S 23:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:8) \[\<Qr fem.], [1 K 7:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:30) \[crpt., v.infr.]) beat, foot, anvil, occurrence;—abs. ´מ [Gn 2:23](https://ref.ly/Gen2:23) \+; פָּעַם [46:30](https://ref.ly/Gen46:30) \+; du. פַּעֲמַיִם [Gn 27:36](https://ref.ly/Gen27:36) \+, etc.; pl. פְּעָמִים [33:3](https://ref.ly/Gen33:3) \+; also (concr.) cstr. פַּעֲמֵי Ju [5:28](https://ref.ly/Judg5:28) \+; sf. פְּעָמַי [Psalm 57:7](https://ref.ly/Ps57:7), [119:133](https://ref.ly/Ps119:133), etc.; פַּעֲמֹתָיו (only **1 c**) [Ex 25:12](https://ref.ly/Exod25:12) \+ 2 t.;—**1\. a.** poet., *hoof\-beat* Ju [5:28](https://ref.ly/Judg5:28); late, of human *footfall, footstep,* fig. [La 2:16](https://ref.ly/Lam2:16), [3:46](https://ref.ly/Lam3:46), [Psalm 22:14](https://ref.ly/Ps22:14); lit. Ez [2:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:8). **b.** to speak Ju [11:35](https://ref.ly/Judg11:35), [11:36](https://ref.ly/Judg11:36) (utter a vow, \+ ´אֶל־י); [Jb 35:16](https://ref.ly/Job35:16); hence \= *utter,* אֲשֶׁר פָּצוּ שְׁפָתַי [Psalm 66:14](https://ref.ly/Ps66:14); of bird chirping פֹּצֶה פֶה [Is 10:14](https://ref.ly/Isa10:14) (in fig.). **2\.** *snatch away, set free* (Aram. loan\-word), only Psalm 144, \+ הִצִּיל c. מִן v:[7](https://ref.ly/Ps144:7); v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ps144:11); ´פ c. מִן v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ps144:10).

## פקע (id: BDB07813)

פקע (√ of foll., mng. dub.; NH פָּקַע *split, spring off;* cf. Targum Ithpe., Syr. ܦܩܰܥ *burst, crash;* NH פְּקַעַת is *ball, roll*, פְּקִיעַ *wick*, הִפְקִיעַ denom.; Ar. فَقَعَ is *be yellow*, also *break wind*, II. crack finger\-joints, etc.; [Ecclus 46:17](https://ref.ly/Sir46:17) פקע *crash;* Targum פְּקַע *roll noisily;* Syr. ܦܰܩܥܳܐ *crash, crack*).

> \[Str6497] † פְּקָעִים **n.m.**:[1 K 7:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:24) **pl.** carved wood or metal ornaments, either ball\-, knob\-shaped (Löwp. 332 f.) of gourd\-shaped (so most; cf. foll.; Syr. ܦܩܺܝܥܶܐ *flowers* \[Lexx]);—[1 K 6:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:18), [7:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:24), [7:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:24).

> \[Str6498] † פַּקֻּעֹת **n.\[f.] pl.** gourds (*wild cucumbers, citrullus colocynthis*, with purgative properties, acc. to PostHastings DB ii. 250 DeRi HWB 278 f. LXX κολοκυνθις; \> *momordica* (or *ecballium*) *elaterium*, which is not a *vine;* cf. Syr. ܦܰܩܽܘܥܳܐ, fruit of *colocynthis* (or the similar *cucumis prophetarum*, Löwl. c. Brock);—cstr. שָדֶה ´פ [2 K 4:39](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:39).

> \[Str6499] פַּר v. פרר

## I פָּרַד (id: BDB07822)

\[Str6504] † I. \[פָּרַד] **vb. divide** (NH *id., divide, separate;* so Syr. in der. spec. and deriv.; Ar. فَرَدَ *be single, sole*, also (Dozy) *unfold, unroll*):—**Qal** *Pt. pass.* fpl. פְּרֻדוֺת Ez [1:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:11) *divided*, i.e. spread, of wings. **Niph.** *Pf*. 3 pl. נִפְרְדוּ [Gn 10:5](https://ref.ly/Gen10:5), [10:32](https://ref.ly/Gen10:32), נִפְרָדוּ [2 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:23); *Impf.*, יִפָּרֵד [Gn 2:10](https://ref.ly/Gen2:10), [Pr 19:4](https://ref.ly/Prov19:4), etc.; *Imv.* ms. הִפָּ֫רֶד נָא [Gn 13:9](https://ref.ly/Gen13:9); *Inf. cstr.* הִפָּֽרֶ־ד לוֺט v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen13:14); *Pt.* נִפְרָד Ju [4:11](https://ref.ly/Judg4:11), [Pr 18:1](https://ref.ly/Prov18:1), נִפְּרָדִים [Ne 4:13](https://ref.ly/Neh4:13); —**1\.** *divide, separate* (intrans.), [Gn 2:10](https://ref.ly/Gen2:10) (of river, *dividing* into branches); of one man *separating* from another, c. מֵעַל [13:9](https://ref.ly/Gen13:9), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Gen13:11), מֵעִם v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen13:14) (all J), from others, מִן Ju [4:11](https://ref.ly/Judg4:11); recipr. [Gn 25:23](https://ref.ly/Gen25:23) (J; of sons of Rebekah representing nations, c. מִמֵּעַיִח in constr. praegn. \[Ges§ 199 ff], i.e. divided (hostile) from birth); abs. [Pr 18:1](https://ref.ly/Prov18:1); of peoples separating from (מִן) parent stock [10:5](https://ref.ly/Prov10:5), [10:32](https://ref.ly/Prov10:32) (P). **2\.** *be divided, separated*, [2 S 1:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:23), [Ne 4:13](https://ref.ly/Neh4:13); of loss of friendship [Pr 19:4](https://ref.ly/Prov19:4). **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יְפָרֵָדוּ [Ho 4:14](https://ref.ly/Hos4:14) *make a separation*, i.e. go apart (in company with \[עִם] harlots, cf. RS Sem. i. 436, 2nd ed. 455\). **Pu.** *Pt. divided*, מְפֻזָּר וּמְפֹרָד בֵּין הָעַמִּים…עַם [Est 3:8](https://ref.ly/Esth3:8) **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הִפְרִיד [Gn 30:40](https://ref.ly/Gen30:40); *Impf.* יַפְרִיד [Ru 1:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:17), [Pr 18:18](https://ref.ly/Prov18:18), 3 mpl. וַיַּפְרִדוּ [2 K 2:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:11); *Inf. cstr.* sf. בְּהַפְרִידוֺ [Dt 32:8](https://ref.ly/Deut32:8); *Pt.* מַפְרִיד [Pr 16:28](https://ref.ly/Prov16:28), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Prov17:9); —**1\.** *divide, separate* [Gn 30:40](https://ref.ly/Gen30:40) (JE) [Dt 32:8](https://ref.ly/Deut32:8), of separating friends [Pr 16:28](https://ref.ly/Prov16:28), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Prov17:9). **2\.** *make a division, separation*, between (בֵּין), [2 K 2:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:11), [Ru 1:17](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:17); of parting disputants (c. בֵּין) [Pr 18:18](https://ref.ly/Prov18:18). **Hithp.** *Pf.* 3 pl. וְהִתְפָּֽרְדוּ [Psalm 22:15](https://ref.ly/Ps22:15); *Impf.* יִתְפָּֽרְדוּ [92:10](https://ref.ly/Ps92:10), רָדוּ\- [Jb 4:11](https://ref.ly/Job4:11), [41:9](https://ref.ly/Job41:9); —*be divided, separated, from each other;* of scales of crocodile (c. neg.) [Jb 41:9](https://ref.ly/Job41:9), of bones, \= be loosened at the joint, [Psalm 22:15](https://ref.ly/Ps22:15) (fig. of helplessness); \= be dispersed [Jb 4:11](https://ref.ly/Job4:11), [Psalm 92:10](https://ref.ly/Ps92:10).

> \[Str6507] † \[פְּרֻדָה] **n.f.** grain of seed (?) (prop., si vera l., the *separated;* cf. Syr. ܦܶܪܕܳܐ, ܦܪܳܕܳܐ, Talm. פְּרִידָא, *pebble, berry*);—only pl. פְּרֻדוֺת [Jo 1:17](https://ref.ly/Joel1:17).

H.; Gi Baer in marg.);—*mule*, ridden by princes [2 S 13:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:29), in battle [18:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:9), [18:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:9), [18:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:9); cf. (later, \+ horses, etc.) [Zc 14:15](https://ref.ly/Zech14:15), [Is 66:20](https://ref.ly/Isa66:20); royal gift [1 K 10:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:25) \= [2 Ch 9:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:24); royal property [1 K 18:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:5); in trade of Tyre Ez [27:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:14); beast of burden, מַשָּא צֶמֶד פְּרָדִים [2 K 5:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:17) *the load of a pair of mules*, of earth, [1 Ch 12:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:40); property of returning exiles [Ezr 2:66](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:66) \= [Ne 7:68](https://ref.ly/Neh7:68); as refractory [Psalm 32:9](https://ref.ly/Ps32:9) (in sim.).

> \[Str6506] † פִּרְדָּה **n.f.** she\-mule;—´פ ridden by king [1 K 1:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:33), cstr. פִּרְדַּת הַמֶּלֶח v:[38](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:38), v:[44](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:44).

> \[Str6508] † פַּרְדֵּס **n.\[m.]** preserve, park (loan\-word from Zend *pairi\-daêza, enclosure*, Spieg Haug in EwJahrb. v. 162 f., cf. SpiegAvesta i. 293; Pers. پاليز *pâlêz* LagGes. Abh. 75, 211, Kurd. *parês* NöZMG xxxvi (1882\), 182; hence Arm. *paṛtêz* LagArm. Stud. § 1878, late As. *pardisu* MeissnZA vi. 290, Ar. فِرْدَوْسٌ; Gk. παράδεισος; cf. also DlPa 95 ff. DIGn 2, 8\);—´הַפּ [Ne 2:8](https://ref.ly/Neh2:8) (containing trees); cstr. פַּרְדֵּס רִמּוֺנִים Ct [4:13](https://ref.ly/Song4:13) (fruit\-trees and costly plants); pl. פַּרְדֵּסִים [Ec 2:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:5) (\+ גַּנּוֺת).

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Samuel 18:9; Nehemiah 7:68; 2 Samuel 13:29; Zechariah 14:15; 1 Kings 1:33; 1 Kings 18:5; Psalms 32:9; Isaiah 66:20; Ezra 2:66; 1 Kings 1:44; 2 Kings 5:17; 2 Chronicles 9:24; 1 Kings 10:25; 1 Chronicles 12:40; Ezekiel 27:14; 1 Kings 1:38; Song of Songs 4:13; Nehemiah 2:8; Ecclesiastes 2:5


## פָּרָה (id: BDB07829)

\[Str6509,6500] † \[פָּרָה] **vb.** bear fruit, be fruitful (NH \= BH 1, פְּרִי \= BH; Ph. פר *fruit;* Eth. ፈርየ፡ ፈረየ፡ *blossom, bear fruit;* As. *pir'u, posterity;* cf. also Targum פֵּירָא Syr. ܦܶܐܪܳܐ *fruit*, and BaZMG xii (1887\), 604\):—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. פָּרוּ [Ex 1:7](https://ref.ly/Exod1:7); 1 pl. consec. וּפָרִינוּ [Gn 26:22](https://ref.ly/Gen26:22), etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִפְרֶה [Is 11:1](https://ref.ly/Isa11:1), etc.; *Imv.* ms. פְּרֵה [Gn 35:11](https://ref.ly/Gen35:11), mpl. פְּרוּ [1:22](https://ref.ly/Gen1:22) \+; *Pt. act.* פֹּרֶה [Dt 29:17](https://ref.ly/Deut29:17), f. פֹּרִיָּה Ez [19:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:10), [Is 17:6](https://ref.ly/Isa17:6), פֹּרָת (Ges§ 80 g; for \*פֹּרַיַת LagBN 81\) [Gn 49:22](https://ref.ly/Gen49:22); [49:22](https://ref.ly/Gen49:22); —**1\.** of men and animals, [Ex 23:30](https://ref.ly/Exod23:30) (E) [Gn 26:22](https://ref.ly/Gen26:22) (J); esp. \+ רבה [Je 3:16](https://ref.ly/Jer3:16), [23:3](https://ref.ly/Jer23:3), Ez [36:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:11), [Gn 35:11](https://ref.ly/Gen35:11), [47:27](https://ref.ly/Gen47:27) (P), and שָׁרַץ (P) [Gn 8:17](https://ref.ly/Gen8:17), [9:7](https://ref.ly/Gen9:7), [Ex 1:7](https://ref.ly/Exod1:7), ´פְּרוּ וּרְבוּ וּמִלְאוּ וגו [Gn 1:22](https://ref.ly/Gen1:22), [1:28](https://ref.ly/Gen1:28), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Gen9:1). **2\.** of vine, גֶּפֶן פֹּרִיָּה [Is 32:12](https://ref.ly/Isa32:12) *fruitful vine*, so [Psalm 128:3](https://ref.ly/Ps128:3) (sim. of wife); cf. (in fig. of Isr.) Ez [19:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:10); in Mess. prediction [Is 11:1](https://ref.ly/Isa11:1) a shoot from his \[Jesse's] roots *shall bear fruit;* c. acc. ראֹשׁ ´שֹׁרֶשׁ פ fig., [Dt 29:17](https://ref.ly/Deut29:17) *a root bearing gall* (as its fruit); יִפְרוּ יֶשַׁע [Is 45:8](https://ref.ly/Isa45:8) is dub., read perh. 3 fs. תֵּפֶר *let* earth *be fruitful in salvation* (Oort al.); *Pt.* f., as subst., הַפֹּרַיָּה *the fruit\-bearer*, poet. for tree, [Is 17:6](https://ref.ly/Isa17:6) (cf. \[סָעִיף] **2**); so בֵּן פֹּרָת [Gn 49:22](https://ref.ly/Gen49:22), [49:22](https://ref.ly/Gen49:22) Joseph is *son of a fruit\-bearer*, i.e. a fruitful bough. **Hiph. 1\.** *cause to bear fruit*, **2\.** *make fruitful*, subj. ´י c. acc. of man or people: **1\.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. הִפְרַנִי [Gn 41:52](https://ref.ly/Gen41:52) (E). **2\.** in P, וְהִפְרֵתִ֫י [Gn 17:6](https://ref.ly/Gen17:6), [17:20](https://ref.ly/Gen17:20) (רֵיתֵ֫י\-), \+ הִרְבָּה [Lv 26:9](https://ref.ly/Lev26:9) (H); *Impf.* 3 ms. juss. יַפְרְ וְיַרְבְּ [Gn 28:3](https://ref.ly/Gen28:3), וַיֶּפֶר [Psalm 105:24](https://ref.ly/Ps105:24); *Pt.* הִנְנִי מַפְרְ וְהִרְבִּיתְִ [Gn 48:4](https://ref.ly/Gen48:4). **3\.** *shew fruitfullness, bear fruit* (Ges§ 53 c. d, g): *Impf.* 3 ms. יַפְרִיא [Ho 13:15](https://ref.ly/Hos13:15) (as if from פרא).

> \[Str6529] פְּרִי:119 **n.m.**:[Ho 14:9](https://ref.ly/Hos14:9) fruit;—abs. ´פ [Ho 9:16](https://ref.ly/Hos9:16) \+, פֶָּרִי [Je 12:12](https://ref.ly/Jer12:12) \+; esp. cstr. פְּרִי [Gn 4:3](https://ref.ly/Gen4:3) \+; sf. פִּרְיִי [Pr 8:19](https://ref.ly/Prov8:19), פֶּרְיְ [Ho 14:9](https://ref.ly/Hos14:9), פִּרְיוֺ [Gn 3:6](https://ref.ly/Gen3:6) \+, וּפֶרְיְכֶם Ez [36:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:8), פְּרִיהֶם [Am 9:14](https://ref.ly/Amos9:14), פִּרְיָם [La 2:20](https://ref.ly/Lam2:20) \+, פִּרְיָמוֺ [Psalm 21:11](https://ref.ly/Ps21:11), פְּרִיהֶן [Je 29:28](https://ref.ly/Jer29:28), פִּרְיָן v:[5](https://ref.ly/Jer29:5), etc.;—**1\.** פְּרִי הָאֲרָמָה [Gn 4:3](https://ref.ly/Gen4:3) (J), *fruit of the ground*, of produce in gen., so [Dt 7:13](https://ref.ly/Deut7:13) \+ 9 t. Dt \+ 3 t.; הָאֶרֶץ ´פ [Nu 13:20](https://ref.ly/Num13:20), [13:26](https://ref.ly/Num13:26), [13:27](https://ref.ly/Num13:27) (JE) \+ 5 t., cf. Ez [25:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:4); *fruit* of vineyard [2 K 19:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:29) \= [Is 37:30](https://ref.ly/Isa37:30), [Zc 8:12](https://ref.ly/Zech8:12), [Is 65:21](https://ref.ly/Isa65:21), Ct [8:11](https://ref.ly/Song8:11), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Song8:12), in fig. [Ho 10:11](https://ref.ly/Hos10:11) \+ 5 t. \+ Ez [17:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:14) (but del. LXX Co al.); esp. הָעֵץ ´פ [Gn 3:2](https://ref.ly/Gen3:2), [3:3](https://ref.ly/Gen3:3), [3:6](https://ref.ly/Gen3:6), [Ex 10:15](https://ref.ly/Exod10:15) (all J) \+ 21 t., also, in fig., [Am 2:9](https://ref.ly/Amos2:9), [Ho 9:16](https://ref.ly/Hos9:16) \+ 4 t. \+ Ez [17:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:23) (read פּאֹרָה *branches* Co al.); of gardens [Am 9:14](https://ref.ly/Amos9:14), [Je 29:5](https://ref.ly/Jer29:5), [29:28](https://ref.ly/Jer29:28), fig. Ct [4:12](https://ref.ly/Song4:12), [4:16](https://ref.ly/Song4:16); עֵץ פְּרִי is *fruit\-tree* [Gn 1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11), [Psalm 148:9](https://ref.ly/Ps148:9), cf. [Ec 2:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:5); אֶרֶץ פְּרִי [Psalm 107:34](https://ref.ly/Ps107:34); פְּרִי תְּבוּאָךְ v:[37](https://ref.ly/Ps107:37) *fruit of* (consisting in) *a crop;* [Psalm 72:16](https://ref.ly/Ps72:16) is dub.; Che Du interpr. as **2**; Bae prop. יִפְרוּ. **2\.** \= *offspring: fruit* of womb (בֶּטֶן) [Gn 30:2](https://ref.ly/Gen30:2), [Dt 7:13](https://ref.ly/Deut7:13) \+ 9 t., cf. [La 2:20](https://ref.ly/Lam2:20), [Psalm 21:11](https://ref.ly/Ps21:11); of cattle (בְּהֵמָה) [Dt 28:4](https://ref.ly/Deut28:4), [28:11](https://ref.ly/Deut28:11), [28:51](https://ref.ly/Deut28:51), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Deut30:9); of serpent, fig. of power conquering Philistia [Is 14:29](https://ref.ly/Isa14:29). **3\.** fig. of *fruit* of actions, i.e. their consequences: good [Is 3:10](https://ref.ly/Isa3:10), [Psalm 58:12](https://ref.ly/Ps58:12); bad [Ho 10:13](https://ref.ly/Hos10:13), [Pr 1:31](https://ref.ly/Prov1:31), [Mi 7:13](https://ref.ly/Mic7:13), of thoughts [Je 6:19](https://ref.ly/Jer6:19); of result of removing sin [Is 27:9](https://ref.ly/Isa27:9); result of labour (*fruit* of hands) [Pr 31:16](https://ref.ly/Prov31:16), [31:31](https://ref.ly/Prov31:31); product of works of ´י [Psalm 104:13](https://ref.ly/Ps104:13), of wisdom [Pr 8:19](https://ref.ly/Prov8:19); צְדָקָה ´פ [Am 6:12](https://ref.ly/Amos6:12), cf. צַדִּיק ´פ [Pr 11:30](https://ref.ly/Prov11:30) (rd. צֶדֶק LXX Hi Toy); of doings (i.e. course of life, or character) [Je 17:10](https://ref.ly/Jer17:10), [21:14](https://ref.ly/Jer21:14), [32:19](https://ref.ly/Jer32:19); פִי־אִישׁ ´פ i.e. speech [Pr 12:14](https://ref.ly/Prov12:14), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Prov13:2), [18:20](https://ref.ly/Prov18:20); *fruit* of arrogance [Is 10:12](https://ref.ly/Isa10:12) is arrogant speech; *fruit* of tongue [Pr 18:21](https://ref.ly/Prov18:21) \= consequences of speech.

> \[Str6501,6510–11] I, II. פָּרָה v. III. פרר. פֶּרֶה v. פֶּרֶא. p. 825, 831

> \[Str2661,6512] \[פֵּרָה], פֵּרוֺת [Is 2:20](https://ref.ly/Isa2:20) v. \[חֲפַרְפָּרָה] p. 344\.

> \[Str6513] † פֻּרָה **n.pr.m.** Ju [7:10](https://ref.ly/Judg7:10), [7:11](https://ref.ly/Judg7:11), Φαρα.

> \[Str6516] † פַּרְוָיִם **n.pr.loc.** ´זְהַב פ [2 Ch 3:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:6), Φαρουαιμ; in Arabia ? SprengerAlte Geogr. Ar. 54 f. identif. with *Farwa* in Yemen, GlSkizze ii. 347 (less prob.) with *Sâḳ el Farwain* in Yemama (Nejd).

> \[Str6503] † \[פַּרְוֶר] **n.\[m.]** structure (colonnade ?) attached to W. side of Solomon's temple; pl. פַּרְוָרִים [2 K 23:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:11); appar. same word in sg. פַּרְבָּר [1 Ch 26:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:18), [26:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:18) (all c. art.) (Thes cp. Pers. فَرْوْءر *open kiosk, summer\-house* (lit. *light\-bearer*). whence perhaps ´פ as loan\-word, cf. Mishn. פַּרְוָוד, פַּרְוָאר, Targum פַּרְוָורָא *suburd* (made up of *villas, summer\-houses* Thes); ´פ then post\-ex. insertion in [2 K 23:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:11); cf. further Dr in HastingsDB).

> \[Str6518] † \[פֶּ֫רֶז or פָּרָז] **n.\[m.]** פְרָזָו Hb [3:14](https://ref.ly/Hab3:14), text and meaning dub.; *warriors* (Vulgate Targum) or *leaders* (LXX δυναστῶν cf. SyrVer Thes We) would suit context.

## פרז (id: BDB07837)

פרז (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. فَرَزَ *remove, separate* فَرَزٌ *depressed ground between hills;* NH הִפְרִיז *extend* border *exceed* limit; פָּרוּז \= BH פְּרָזִי).

> \[Str6519,6520] † \[פְּרָזָה] **n.f.** open region, hamlet;—only pl. פְּרָזוֺת: ´אֶרֶץ פ Ez [38:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:11) *a land of hamlets* (opp. חוֺמָה etc.); תֵּשֵׁב יְרוּשָׁלַם ´פ [Zc 2:8](https://ref.ly/Zech2:8) *as open regions shall Jerusalem sit* (יָשַׁב **4**); ´עָרֵי הַפּ [Est 9:19](https://ref.ly/Esth9:19) (opp. שׁוּשָׁן v:[18](https://ref.ly/Esth9:18)); perh. Ju [5:7](https://ref.ly/Judg5:7) (´פ for פְּרָזוֺן, cf. SyrVer Targum GFM).

> \[Str6521] † פְּרָזִי **n.\[m.]** hamlet\-dweller;—coll. c. art. ´כֹּפֶר הַפּ [1 S 6:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:18) (opp. עִיר מִבְצָר), ´עָרֵי הַפּ [Dt 3:5](https://ref.ly/Deut3:5) (opp. ´עָרִים בְּצֻרֹת חוֺמָה וגו); pl. הַפְּרָזִים [Est 9:19](https://ref.ly/Esth9:19) Qr (Kt הפרוזים).

> \[Str6520,6518,6519] † פְּרָזוֺן **n.\[m.]** dub.; ´פ Ju [5:7](https://ref.ly/Judg5:7); sf. פִּרְזוֺנוֺ v:[11](https://ref.ly/Judg5:11) (LagBN 119\), poss. coll. *rural population, rustics*, Bachm Bu (the latter as gen. obj. after צִדְקֹת *righteous acts to the peasants*; \> LXX Thes Be *leaders* and *leadership* (cf. \[פֶּרֶז]; text very uncertain; on v:[7](https://ref.ly/Judg5:7) see \[פְּרָזָה] supr.

> \[Str6522] פְּרִזִּי:23 **adj.gent.** Perizzite (poss., but not certainly, connected with above √);—only ´הַפּ as n.pr.coll. ὁ (οἱ) Φερεζαῖος (\-αῖοι), of ancient inhab. of Canaan, \+ הַכְּנַעֲנִי \[q.v.] [Gn 13:7](https://ref.ly/Gen13:7), [34:30](https://ref.ly/Gen34:30) (J), Ju [1:4](https://ref.ly/Judg1:4), [1:5](https://ref.ly/Judg1:5); also in list of peoples dispossessed by Israel [Gn 15:20](https://ref.ly/Gen15:20), [Ex 3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8) \+ 17 t.

* **Associated Passages:** Judges 1:4; Judges 1:5; Judges 5:11; Genesis 34:30; Esther 9:18; Genesis 15:20; Zechariah 2:8; 1 Samuel 6:18; Exodus 3:8; Genesis 13:7; Esther 9:19; Judges 5:7; Deuteronomy 3:5; Ezekiel 38:11


## I פָּרַח (id: BDB07842)

\[Str6524] † I. פָּרַח **vb.** bud, sprout, shoot (NT Hiph. \= BH **Hiph. 2**; Ecclus פרח **Qal** \= *flourish*, **Hiph.** causat., [49:10](https://ref.ly/Sir49:10), [40:19](https://ref.ly/Sir40:19); Ar. فَرِخَ II. *hatch*, also *sprout*, فَرْخٌ *young* of bird (v. אֶפְרֹחַ infr.), *twig, sprout;* As. *pirḫu*, n. *sprout*;—on mng. cf. ## I פרע (id: BDB07864)

I. פרע (√ of foll.; cf. perh. Ar. ܦܪܣܐ *overtop*, fig. *excel;* فَرَعَ *noble, eminent man;* Sab. פרע *be lofty* OsZMG xix (1865\), 178 f., פרע *highest part* SabDenkm90, fig.*best*, DHMib., 32 f. Jüd. Gesch. (2\), 97; Held.(2\). 123 al., *for the loosing of locks* \[II. פֶּרַע], in vow of war; on these and other views v. esp. GFMad loc.; conj. also by LambertREêJ xxiv. 140 GrimmeZMG 1(1896\), 572 CheJQ July 1899, 561 \[reading ברכו]); cstr. מֵראֹשׁ פַּרְעוֺת אוֺיֵב [Dt 32:42](https://ref.ly/Deut32:42) *from the head of leaders of the foe* LXX Di Steuern al.; Kn Ke Dr al. *from the long\-haired heads of the foe*.

## II פרץ (id: BDB07881)

II. פרץ (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. فَرَضَ *notch, make mark by notching;* فُرْضَةٌ *gap* by which boats ascend, unload, or are stationed; فَارِضٌ *mouth* of river or inlet).

## פָּרַק (id: BDB07883)

\[Str6561] † \[פָּרַק] **vb.** tear apart, away (NH *remove* (load, etc.), Pi.*separate, take to pieces;* Ar. فَرَقَ *split, divide;*; Targum פְּרַק esp. *redeem, rescue;* Syr. ܦܪܰܩ *withdraw* (intrans.), also *remove, rescue;* Eth. ፈረቀ: *set free*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms וּפָרַקְדָמתּ֫ [Gn 27:40](https://ref.ly/Gen27:40); *Impf.* 3 ms sf. וַיִּפְרְקֵנזּ [Psalm 136:24](https://ref.ly/Ps136:24); *Pt. act.* פֹּרֵק [La 5:8](https://ref.ly/Lam5:8), [Psalm 7:3](https://ref.ly/Ps7:3) *tear away* yoke from pff (מֵעַל neck [Gn 27:40](https://ref.ly/Gen27:40) (J); *snatck* from (מִן) foes, \= *rescue* (Aram.) [La 5:8](https://ref.ly/Lam5:8), [Psalm 136:24](https://ref.ly/Ps136:24); so abs. [Psalm 7:3](https://ref.ly/Ps7:3) (prefixing זֵין(וְ), Targum SyrVer Che Du We), \> Hup Bae al. *snatch away* as prey. **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms יְפָרֵק [Zc 11:16](https://ref.ly/Zech11:16) *he shall tear off* their hoofs; *Imv.* mpl. פָּֽרְקוּ [Ex 32:2](https://ref.ly/Exod32:2) (E) *tear off* the golden ear\-tings; *Pt.* מְפָרֵק [1 K 19:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:11) a great wind *rending* mts. (‖ מְשַׁבֵּר). **Hithp.** *tear off* (for, i.e. from, oneself Ges§ 54 f); *Impf.* 3 mpl. וַיִּתְפָּֽרְקוּ [Ex 32:3](https://ref.ly/Exod32:3) (E), c. acc. אֶת־נִזְמֵי הַזָּהָב; *Imv.* mpl. הִתְפָּרָקוּ v:[24](https://ref.ly/Exod32:24) (E; obj. om.); *pass. be broken off, Pf.* 3 pl. הִתְפָּֽרְקוּ Ez [19:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:12) (branches).

> \[Str6563] † פֶּ֫רֶק **n.\[m.] 1\.** parting of ways, Ob:[14](https://ref.ly/Obad1:14). **2\.** plunder (as *snatched away*), [Na 3:1](https://ref.ly/Nah3:1).

> \[Str6564] † \[פָּרָק] **n.\[m.] fragment**;—cstr. פְּרַק פִּגֻּלִים [Is 65:4](https://ref.ly/Isa65:4) Kt, but read מְרַק, v. מָרָק.

> \[Str4665] † \[מַפְרֶ֫קֶת] **n.f.** neck (*dividing* head from body);—sf. וַתִּשָׁבֵר מַפְרַקְתּוֺ [1 S 4:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:18).

[Psalm 32:1](https://ref.ly/Ps32:1); pardons (סלח) [1 K 8:50](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:50); passes over (עבר על, cf. (of priest) ´וְכִפֶּר עַל־הַקֹּדֶשׁ מִפ [Lv 16:16](https://ref.ly/Lev16:16), and confession of ´פ over (על) goat v:[21](https://ref.ly/Lev16:21); —God blots out (מחה) [Is 43:25](https://ref.ly/Isa43:25), [44:22](https://ref.ly/Isa44:22), [Psalm 51:3](https://ref.ly/Ps51:3); ´וּפ אַל־תִּזְכֹּר [25:7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:7); delivers from, הציל מן [39:9](https://ref.ly/Ps39:9).—[Jb 35:15](https://ref.ly/Job35:15) read פֶּשַׁע for פַשׁ q. v. **4\.** *guilt of transgression* (cf. עון **2**), ´פ *without* (guilt of) transgression [Jb 33:9](https://ref.ly/Job33:9), [34:6](https://ref.ly/Job34:6); ´לא פ [Psalm 59:4](https://ref.ly/Ps59:4); עלינו ´פ Ez [33:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:10); כבד ´עליה פ [Is 24:20](https://ref.ly/Isa24:20); ´בְּרֹב פ [Psalm 5:11](https://ref.ly/Ps5:11); ´נקיתי מפ [19:14](https://ref.ly/Ps19:14); יִפַּמְּאוּ: *defile themselves with all (the guilt of) transgressions* Ez [14:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:11), [37:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:23); ´בּצְרוֺר פ:חָתֻם [Jb 14:17](https://ref.ly/Job14:17). **5\.** *punishment for transgression*, [Dn 8:12](https://ref.ly/Dan8:12), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Dan8:13), [9:24](https://ref.ly/Dan9:24), cf. try עון **3\.** **6\.** *offering for transgression*, האתן בכורי פשׁעי [Mi 6:7](https://ref.ly/Mic6:7) *shall I give my first\-born as an offering for my transgression* (cf. חטאת **4**).

## פָּתָה (id: BDB07932)

\[Str6601] \[פָּתָה] **vb.** be spacious, wide, open (Aram. פְּתָא *be spacious*, ܦܬܳܐ *be spacious, abundant*; cf. Ar.فَتُوَ, فَتِىَ *be youthful, in prime of life,* فَتًى *young man, one in prime of life* (development of various meanings from √ not wholly clear, cf. NöZMG xi (1886\), 735\)) ;—**Qal** *Pt.* פֹּתֶה [Pr 20:19](https://ref.ly/Prov20:19) poss. *one open* as to lips, but v. פָּתָה denom. infr. **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. juss. ´יַפְתְּ א לְיֶפֶת [Gn 9:27](https://ref.ly/Gen9:27) *may God make wide for Japhet* (give him an extensive inheritance).—*Pf.* poss. [Pr 24:28](https://ref.ly/Prov24:28) (reading וְהִפְתִּיתָ בִּשְפָתֶָיְ, *make wide with* (open wide) *thy lips*, for MT ´יַהֲפִתִּיתָ בִּש, so SS ; but cf. פָּתָה denom. **Pi.**).

834:b. \[פָּתָה] **Niph., Pi.**: [Je 20:7](https://ref.ly/Jer20:7) rather *be persuaded, persuade;* **Pu.** [Je 20:10](https://ref.ly/Jer20:10) *be beguiled*.

Pr; פְּתָיִים [119:130](https://ref.ly/Ps119:130), [Pr 22:3](https://ref.ly/Prov22:3); פְּתָיִם [1:22](https://ref.ly/Prov1:22), [1:32](https://ref.ly/Prov1:32); —*simple,* as subst. concr.: open to the instruction of wisdom or folly, [Pr 9:4](https://ref.ly/Prov9:4), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Prov9:16); believing every word [14:15](https://ref.ly/Prov14:15); lacking עָרְטָה [1:14](https://ref.ly/Prov1:14), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Prov8:5), [19:25](https://ref.ly/Prov19:25); needing בין [Psalm 119:130](https://ref.ly/Ps119:130), חכטה [19:8](https://ref.ly/Ps19:8), [Pr 21:11](https://ref.ly/Prov21:11); in good sense, שׁמר ´פתאים י [Psalm 116:8](https://ref.ly/Ps116:8) ´י *preserveth ths simple\-minded*; but usu. tendency to bad sense ; פתאים love פתי [Pr 1:22](https://ref.ly/Prov1:22); inherit אִוֶּלֶת [14:18](https://ref.ly/Prov14:18), are easily enticed, misled and go back [1:32](https://ref.ly/Prov1:32), [7:7](https://ref.ly/Prov7:7), [9:6](https://ref.ly/Prov9:6), [22:3](https://ref.ly/Prov22:3) \= [27:12](https://ref.ly/Prov27:12); they need atonement Ez [45:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:20) (‖שׁגה).

\[Str6605] † I. פָּתַח **vb.** open (NH *id.;* Ph. פתח; As *pitû, patû;* sab, פתח OsZMG xix (1865\), 197; Ar. فَتَحَ; Eth. ፈትሐ፡; Aram. פְּתַח, ܦܬܰܚ; Nab. Pers. [Psalm 105:20](https://ref.ly/Ps105:20), [Je 40:4](https://ref.ly/Jer40:4) (c. מִן); c. acc. בְּנֵי תְמוּתָה [Psalm 102:21](https://ref.ly/Ps102:21). **2\.** *loosen* (and remove) sack\-cloth [Is 20:2](https://ref.ly/Isa20:2) (c. מֵעַל ), [Psalm 30:12](https://ref.ly/Ps30:12); armour [1 K 20:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:11) (obj. om.; opp. חָגַר) ; bonds [Jb 12:18](https://ref.ly/Job12:18), [Psalm 116:16](https://ref.ly/Ps116:16), cf. VogelstLand wrirthschaft 38, As. *puttû*).**3\.** open gates [Is 60:11](https://ref.ly/Isa60:11), doors [Jb 41:6](https://ref.ly/Job41:6) (fig. of crocodile's jaws); ear [Is 48:8](https://ref.ly/Isa48:8) (rd. prob. either פִּתַּחְתִּי, פָּתַחְתִּי \[LXX Du Marti], or נִפְתְּחָה \[Gr Che Kit in Di] ; buds Ct [7:13](https://ref.ly/Song7:13) (of blossom; obj. om.; or intrans., as sts. > \[Str6608] † פֵּ֫תַח **n.m.** opening, unfolding ;—cstr. יָאִיר [Psalm 119:130](https://ref.ly/Ps119:130), so LXX and most; \> Symm Jer Bae פֶּתַח (\= *thy words as a doorway*).

> \[Str6610] †פִּתְחוֺן **n.** \[**m.**] opening;—cstr. פִּתְחוֺן *opening of mouth* in speech or song Ez [16:63](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:63), [29:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:21).

> \[Str6609] † \[פְּתִיחָה] **n.** \[**f.**] drawn sword ;—pl. פְּתִחוֺת [Psalm 55:22](https://ref.ly/Ps55:22) (fig. of words; cf. Ez [21:33](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:28), [Psalm 37:14](https://ref.ly/Ps37:14)).

> \[Str6611] † פְּתַחְיָה **n.pr.m.** **1\.** priest [1 Ch 24:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:16), Εζεκηλ, φεθεια etc. **2\.** Levite [Ezr 10:23](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:23), [Ne 9:5](https://ref.ly/Neh9:5), φαθαια, φεθεια (ς) etc. **3\.** Judahite [Ne 11:24](https://ref.ly/Neh11:24), Παθαια φαθαια.

## פֶּתַע (id: BDB07965)

\[Str6621] † פֶּתַע **subst.** suddenness ( √ unknown: cf. As adv. acc., *suddenly,* [Pr 6:15](https://ref.ly/Prov6:15) (\= [29:1](https://ref.ly/Prov29:1)) פֶּתַע יִשָּׁבֵר, Hb [2:7](https://ref.ly/Hab2:7).
